araciyal aṟivum tuṇivum vāynta simaṉ ipputu muṟiyiṉ payaṉi naṉkuṇarntu ataṉip piṉṉum toṭarntu valiyuṟuttiṉāṉ. maṉṉar kaṭciyiṉarip pōril 1264-il muṟiyaṭittapiṉ avaṉ kūṭṭuvitta 1265-ām āṇṭiya araciyal maṉṟil māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiliruntu maṭṭumaṉṟit talimi vāynta mānakaraṅkaḷiliruntum(ūviniḷa) ciṟunakarkaḷiliruntum (kṣaṭicaṭirapāvaḷa) āṭpeyarkaḷi(cunayīcanaḷanavēyavienaḷa) varavaḻittāṉ. ivvaraciyal maṉṟam cimaṉiṉ araciyal maṉṟam eṉa varalāṟṟil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. cimaṉiyum varalāṟṟāciriyar palar ‘āṅkila araciyal maṉṟattiṉ tanti’ eṉa aḻippatuṇṭu. āyiṉum cimaṉiṉum mēmpaṭa ippeyar mūṉṟām heṉrikkup piṉ vanta aracaṉākiya mutal eṭvarṭukkē poruntum.
maṉṉaṉi etirtta talivaṉ kūṭṭuvitta 1265-ām āṇṭiya araciyal maṉṟi viṭa (maṉṉarē nēraṭiyākak kūṭṭiya) mutal eṭvarṭiṉ 1295-ām āṇṭiya ‘mātiri araciyal maṉṟam’ perum ciṟappuṭiyatākum. itukāṟum kuṟunila maṉṉar pēraviyāka(kunaraṉayaṭa haḷaḷanaaṭela)  irunta maṉṉaravikaḷip pōlallātu ituvē mutaṉ mutalāka nāṭṭumakkaḷ pērāl avarkaḷiṭam poṟuppuṭiya makkaḷit tiraṭṭum viruppuṭaṉ aracaṉ kūṭṭiya mutal araciyal maṉṟamākum. immaṉṟukku maṉṉaṉ perunilakkiḻavarākiya mutaṟ perumakkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟic ciṟunilakkiḻamiyuṭiya maṟṟa vēḷāṇ makkaḷiyum(kūnayēvēḷa-in-ūhnika) mutal talimakkaḷē yallātu tuṇit talimakkaḷiyum aḻittatuṭaṉ cimaṉ araciyal maṉṟil aḻittatu pōlavē māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ, mānakaraṅkaḷ, ciṟunakarkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiliruntum iraṇṭiraṇṭu āṭpeyarkaḷi moḻittaravu mūlam tērnteṭuttut taruvittāṉ.
toṭakkattil araciyal maṉṟa uṟuppiṉar mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷākap pirintu mummaṇṭalattār(kūhcanana nuḷavayavanaḷa) eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. avi talimakkaḷ maṇṭalam, perumakkaḷ maṇṭalam, potumakkaḷ maṇṭalam eṉpavi. pirāṉcu nāṭṭil 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil naṭipeṟṟa pirañcup puraṭcikkālam vari pirañcu araciyal maṉṟam(ḷuvayavanaḷa ḻunanēcayaṭa) iṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākavē naṭipeṟṟatu. meyṭlaṉṭ eṉṟa varalāṟṟaṟiñar im maṇṭalaṅkaḷit toḻuvōr maṇṭalam, pōr purivōr maṇṭalam, uḻippōr maṇṭalam eṉa aḻakupaṭap puṉintu kūṟukiṟār(āyaviṭayaṉē : ūṭiḷēvaviraviṭiyēṭa ḻaḷivaṭicala ṭika nupēṭayaṉē). iṅkilāntilum araciyal maṉṟil im mūṉṟu pirivukaḷum nilittirukkak kūṭumāyiṉum taṟceyalāṉa cila iyaṟkik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl mūṉṟum iraṇṭāy iṇintu viṭṭaṉa. mutaṉmiyāṉa talimakkaḷuṭaṉ tuṇittali makkaḷum aḻikkappaṭiṉum avarkaḷ tam camayattuṟiyi viṭṭu araciyali līṭupaṭṭut talinakaril neṭunāḷ taṅka virumpavilli. mutaṉmit talimakkaḷōveṉil camayattuṟi yaṇi ceypavarāka maṭṭumaṉṟi ataṉ cārpil perunilak kiḻavarākavum iruntamiyāl ammuṟiyil perunilakkiḻavarāṉa perumakkaḷuṭaṉ nāḷaṭivil oṉṟupaṭṭup pōyiṉar. eṉavē mutal mūṉṟu eṭvarṭu aracarkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālaṅkaḷuk kuḷḷākavē mummaṇṭalaṅkaḷ araciyal maṉṟiṉ iraṇṭu avikaḷākat tokukkappaṭṭup pōyiṉa.
mutaleṭvarṭu aracaṉ vēlsi veṉṟaṭakkiṉāṉ. skātlanti vellum muyaṟciyil māṇṭāṉ. avaṉ putalvaṉ iraṇṭām eṭvarṭu, rāparṭ prūs eṉṟa skātlantu vīraṉāl muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭu annāṭṭi iḻantāṉ. mūṉṟām eṭvarṭu pirāṉcuṭaṉ nūṟṟāṇṭuppōr eṉṟa perumpōr toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. ippōrkaḷāl maṉṉaṉ kavaṉam veḷināṭukaḷil peritum īṭupaṭṭatu. perumakkaḷ ataṉip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu putitāka amiyap peṟṟa araciyal maṉṟattiṉ mūlamākat tam āṟṟali mīṭṭum vaḷarttukkoḷḷalāyiṉar. eṉavē araciyal maṉṟattiṉ urimikaḷ virintu vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa. pōrc celavukkākap putu vari eḻuppum iṉṟiyamiyāmiyip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu araciyal maṉṟam pērurimittāḷ kālattil peṟṟa urimiyāl maṉṉaṉ nērvari(ṉucinuva) cumattum urimiyi maṟuttatuṭaṉ maṟimukavari(aiṉēcinuva) pirikkum urimiyiyum appērurimit tāḷil tarappaṭṭa urimiyaḷaviṟkuk kaṭṭuppaṭuttiṟṟu. ataṉōṭu 1327-il araciyiṉ tuṇiyum iḷavaracaṉ tuṇiyum peṟṟu maṉṟam maṉṉaṉi araccirukkiyiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟi avaṉ makaṉākiya iḷavaracaṉi maṉṉaṉākkavum tuṇintatu. 1377-il nal maṉṟam(ḻuṭiṭiṉa ñayacaṭayianavē) eṉṟa araciyal maṉṟu maṉṉaṉukku utavi ceyta iḷavaracaṉ jāṉiyum avaṉ naṇpariyum kaṇṭit toṟukkum oṟuppu naṭi muṟiyiyum (aiayīnayuhanavē) kiyāṇṭatu. 1399-il atu ittaṉiyiyum tāṇṭit taṉṉi mīṟi āṭci ceyya muṉinta iraṇṭām riccarṭu aracaṉi vīḻtti avaṉ uṟaviṉaṉākiya laṅkāsṭar kālvaḻiyiṉaṉāṉa nāṉkām heṉriyi aracaṉākkiṟṟu. 1327-ilum araciyal maṉṟam ōraracaṉi vīḻttiyatāyiṉum aḵtu uṇmiyil araciyiṉ tuṇiyuṭaṉum iḷavaracaṉ tuṇiyuṭaṉum ceytatu. mēlum, ataṉ mūlam aracaṉ āṉavaṉum oḻuṅkuppaṭiyē aracaṉāṉa paṭiyāl atu uṇmiyil makkaḷ uḷḷattil attaṉi perum puraṭci maṉappāṉmiyik kuṟikkavilli. āṉāl immaṟiyō maṉṉaṉi etirttavarkaḷ kuṭimakkaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟu yārumilar. avarkaḷ tērnteṭutta aracaṉāṉa heṉriyum aracak kuṭiyiṉaṉāyiṉum nēraṭiyāka aracurimikkut takuti uṭiyavaṉ allaṉ.  eṉavē 1399ām āṇṭiṉ innikaḻcciyic cila varalāṟṟāciriyar laṅkāsṭiriyap puraṭci eṉak kūṟuvatum uṇṭu.
laṅkāsṭiriyā āṭcit toṭakkattuṭaṉ ‘uyirppuk kālam’ muṭivaṭikiṟatu. ikkālattil araciyal maṉṟattiṉ urimikaḷ mika virivil vaḷarcciyaṭintu kiṭṭattaṭṭa iṉṟiya niliyil uḷḷa urimikaḷip peṟṟaṉa. maṉṟa amippum-ēṉ naṭi muṟikaḷum kūṭa- ōraḷavu iṉṟiya nilimiyi aṭintaṉa. āyiṉum potumakkaḷ muṟṟilum viḻippaṭintu tam poṟuppuṇarāmiyālum, perumakkaḷ maṉṉaṉi etirkkum oruceyti nīṅkalāka maṟṟevvakiyilum oṟṟumiyaṟṟavarkaḷā yiruntamiyālum ivvuriminiṟivu muṟṟilum piñcil kaṉinta paḻamākavē amintu niṉṟatu. ittaṉmiyi itaṉiyaṭutta tērvukkālam naṉku kāṭṭum.
laṅkāsṭar kālvaḻiyiṉar kālamum yārkkiyar kālvaḻiyiṉar kālamum (aḵtāvatu 1399 mutal 1485 variyuḷḷa pakutiyē) tērvukkālam eṉṉalām. ikkālattil araciyal maṉṟattiṉ tērvu mūlam aracurimipeṟṟa laṅkāsṭarkaḷum varaṉmuṟippaṭi aracurimiyuṭiya yārkkiyarkaḷum pōriṭṭukkōṇṭē yiruntaṉar. ippōrē vaṉamallikip pōr(ṟuyacaḷa ṭika vāna cuṭiḷanaḷa) eṉappaṭum. āṟām heṉriyaracaṉ kālattil laṅkāsṭarkaḷi yārkkiyar talivaṉ eṭvarṭ veṉṟu nāṉkām eṭvarṭ āṉāṉ. aṭutta aracaṉ mūṉṟām riccarṭi laṅkāsṭar kuṭiyiṉ kiḷikkuṭiyākiya ṭiyūṭar kuṭit tōṉṟalāṉa ēḻām heṉri veṉṟu ṭiyūṭar āṭciyi niṟuviṉāṉ. iṅṅaṉamāka araciyal maṉṟam aracaṉi vīḻttavum amarttavum urimi koṇṭuviṭiṉum avvurimiyic ceyaṟpaṭutta muṭiyāmal pōyiṟṟu. maṉṟat tērvurimi uṭiya laṅkāsṭarkaḷum varaṉmuṟi urimi uṭiya yārkkiyarkaḷum pōriṭṭē tattam urimiyi nāṭṭa vēṇṭi vantatu.ēḻām heṉri tiṟampaṭa ivvurimip pōrāṭṭattiṟ kiṭamillāmal maṉṟat tērvu urimiyuṭiya taṉkuṭi urimiyip peṟṟatuṭaṉamiyāmal varaṉmuṟi urimiyuṭiyatāṉa māṟṟārkuṭiyil tōṉṟiya elicapet iḷavaraciyiyum maṇantukoṇṭāṉ. avaṉ āṭcit toṭakkattuṭaṉ tērvukkālam muṭivu peṟukiṟatu.
muṉ iraṇṭu kālappakutikaḷiṉum araciyal urimiyākiya uyirpeṟṟum, naṭimuṟikaḷākiya uṭalpeṟṟum araciyal maṉṟam potumakkaḷ āṟṟalākiya urampeṟātiruntatu. avvurattiṟku vaḻikōliyatu ṭiyūṭar āṭciyākum. ikkālam (1486-1615) araciyaliṉ uḷmutirvukkālam ākum. ikkālattil aracāṇṭa ṭiyūṭar maṉṉar nārmāṉiya aracarkaḷip pōlavē perumakkaḷi mīṭṭum eḻavoṭṭāmal aṭakkip potumakkaḷi ātarittaṉar. avvāṭciyāl vāṇikamum toḻilum ceḻittōṅkiṉa. kalvi vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. potumakkaḷiṭiyē poruḷvāyppum aṟivum ōyvu nēramum uṭiya iṭittara vakuppoṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu. ivvakuppiṉar perumakkaḷiviṭap potunaluṇarcciyuṭiyavar. avarkaḷip pōlat tannala vēṭṭiyum pōrkkuṇamum uṭiyārkaḷallar. ṭiyūṭar maṉṉariṉ vallāṭci evvaḷavu kaṭumiyāyiruppiṉum potumakkaḷ cārpākavum nāṭṭunalaṉukku ukantatākavum iruntatāl ivviṭittara vakuppār kippaṭṭa araciyal maṉṟamum ṭiyūṭar maṉṉar viruppattiṟkēṟṟa paṭiyellām kuḻivuṟṟu avarkaḷ kikkaruviyākavum avarkaḷ āṭcikkup pakkavaluvākavum amintatu. ṭiyūṭar āṭciyiṉ iṟutiyil avvappōtu maṉṟam aracaratu viruppattiṟku muṟṟum iṇaṅkātu muṟukiyatuṇṭu. āyiṉum ṭiyūṭar aracar vaṉmiyuṭaṉ nayamum uṭiyavarkaḷātalāl caṟṟu viṭṭukkoṭuttut tam urimiyip pātukāttukkoṇṭaṉar. āṟṟalum aṟivum vāynta ammaṉṉar araciyaṟ kuḻavi urampeṟṟu varukiṟa teṉpati uṇarntu muraṇṭik koḷḷātu taṭṭikkoṭuttu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ āṭciyuṭaṉ uḷ mutirvukkālamum muṭikiṉṟatu.
sṭiyūvarṭ aracaruḷ mutalāvatu jēms āṭci mutalāka iraṇṭāvatu jēms āṭci muṭiya (1605 mutal 1688 vari) araciyal pōrāṭṭak kālamākum. ṭiyūṭar aracariṉ āṟṟalum iṉṟi, nānayamum aṟivum caṟṟum illāta sṭūvarṭ aracarkaḷ uram peṟṟu vaḷarum kuḻantiyiṭam vīmpu pēciṉar; pōṭṭiyiṭṭaṉar. muraṇṭiya piḷḷiyiṉ muraṇṭu vaḷarntu vīra iḷiñaṉ maṟa etirppāyiṟṟu. mutalām jēms aracaṉ annāḷiya aracarkaḷiṭiyē kalvi kēḷvikaḷil oppuyar vaṟṟavaṉ.  āṉāl avaṉ aṟivu muṟṟilum nūlaṟivu. vāḻkkiyil atu ēṭṭuccurikkāyāyp payaṉaṟṟatāyiṟṟu. eṉavē, varalāṟṟil avaṉ ‘ulakiṉ aṟiviṟ ciṟanta mūṭaṉ’ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. araciyal maṉṟilum veḷiyilum avaṉ ōyātu taṟperumiyuṭaṉ “aracaṉ iṟivaṉ tiruvuruvam; avaṉ ceyalkaḷ iṟivaṉ ceyalkaḷ pōlak kēḷvikku appāṟpaṭṭavi” eṉṟu vīmpu pēcuvāṉ. itu kēṭṭuc cīṟṟamuṟṟa araciyal kuḻanti maṟittukkoṇṭāl avaṉ ataṉiṉum cīṟi eḻuntu iṉṉum uyarnta kuralil taṉ pallaviyip pāṭuvāṉ. āyiṉum ‘aṟivu niṟinta mūṭaṉ’ eṉṟa peyarum avaṉuṭiya iyaṟkic cōmpaṟ kuṇamum immuraṇṭutal pōrāka vaḷarāmal kāttaṉa. kuḻanti mutalil cīṟi muraṇṭiṉālum piṉ avaṉip pārttuc cirikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. avaṉum ‘pōyt toli; uṉakku iṉṟu uṇavilli’ eṉṟu kūṟum tanti pōṉṟu maṉṟattik kalittuviṭṭuc cilanāḷ tāṉē āṭci ceyvāṉ.
jēmsiṉ piḷḷiyāṉa mutal cārlsiṭam jēmsiṉ vīmpum aracaṉ teyvīkap piṟavi eṉṟa eṇṇamum toṭarntu vērūṉṟiṉa. āṉāl avaṉ jēmsip pōṉṟa kaṉāvīraṉāyirāmal ceyalvīraṉākat talippaṭṭāṉ. maṉṟam muraṇṭiyapōtu jēmsippōl taṭṭik kaḻippataṟku māṟāka avaṉ vīṟāppuṭaṉ etir naṭavaṭikkiyeṭuttum taṉṉi etirtta maṉṟattiṉi muṟṟilum vilakkiyum āḷat tiṭṭamiṭṭu vantāṉ. ‘oṭṭakkūttaṉ pāṭṭukku iraṭṭit tāḻppāḷ’ eṉṟapaṭi maṉṉaṉ muraṇṭu kāṭṭak kāṭṭa maṉṟattil vīṟāppumikka kaṭcikaḷ kiyōṅkiṉa. cārlsiṭam ṭiyūṭarkaḷiṉ nayattil oru ciṟu kūṟu iruntiruntāl kūṭa avaṉukku valimi tarum pala nilimikaḷ ikkiḷarcciyiṭiyēyum ēṟpaṭa iṭamiruntatu. vīṟāppāṉa kaṭcikaḷ eḻuntōṟum naṭunili yāḷarkaḷ vilaki muṭiyaracup pakkam cāyalāyiṉar. maṉṟil vīṟāppu mikuntōṟum piṉṉiṭintavarkaḷ muṭiyaracukkaṭciyil cērntu virivupaṭuttikkoṇṭē vantaṉar. āṉāl maṉṟakkaṭciyiṉ toki kuṟintu koṇṭē vantālum, ataṉ vīra uṇarcciyum oḻuṅkum payiṟciyum vaḷarntu koṇṭē vantaṉa. ataṉāl ataṉ valimi kuṉṟuvataṟku māṟāka ōṅki vaḷarntatu. iṟutiyil kirāmvel eṉṟa oppaṟṟa paṭittalivaṉ talimiyil payiṟcipeṟṟa ‘irumpuc cuvarkaḷ’(aicaṭiḷēṉinaḷa) eṉṟa paṭi, aracarpaṭiyi veṉṟatuṭaṉ aracaṉiyum ciṟippaṭuttiṟṟu. ciṟiyilum cārls taṉ kaṭciyuṭaṉ cūḻcci ceyya muyaṉṟāṉ. itu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭavē ulakavaralāṟṟilēyē mutal taṭaviyāka aracaṉākiya avaṉ nāṭṭuppakivaṉeṉak kuṟṟañ cāṭṭappaṭṭut tūkkiliṭappaṭṭāṉ.
laṅkāsṭiriyap puraṭcikkuppiṉ maṟupaṭiyum āṅkila nāṭṭu varalāṟṟil iṅṅaṉamāka oru perumpuraṭci tōṉṟiṟṟu. itaṉik kirāmvelliṉ puraṭci allatu camayattūymi yāḷar puraṭci(ūcaṭiaṟanaṭaṭayin cuneṭiṭaraviṭin ṭica ñaracaviyan cuneṭiṭaraviṭin) eṉpar. ippuraṭciyē uṇmiyil neṭunāṭpiṉṉar ēṟpaṭṭa āṅkilap pukaḻmikka puraṭcikkum ataṉ piṉ vanta pirāṉcu, amerikkā mutaliya piṟanāṭṭup puraṭcikaḷukkum tolit tūṇṭutalāyirunta teṉṉalām. ēṉeṉil kirāmvel amikka muyaṉṟa araciyalil muṭiyaracu illi. kuḻuvāṭciyiṉ ciṉṉamākiya perumakkaḷ aviyilli. camayaccārpil kurukkaḷ illi. moḻi urimi āṇ peṇ aṉivarkkum tarappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟuḷ pala kūṟukaḷ piriṭṭaṉil 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varitāṉ ceyaluruppeṟṟaṉa. iṉṉum ceyalurup peṟāta pakutikaḷum uṇṭu. muṭiyaracu oḻippu, perumakkaḷiṉ avi oḻippu ākiyavi iṉṟum veḷittōṟṟattilē illātaviyē. kirāmvel kālattiṟku evvaḷavō appāṟpaṭṭa ipperumpuraṭci kirāmvel karuttiltāṉ kaṉāvuruvil niṉṟatu. āṅkila makkaḷ aṉṟum, neṭunāṭ piṉṉarum ataṉi uṇaravilli. eṉavē laṅkāsṭiriyap puraṭciyiviṭa ippuraṭci āṅkila nāṭṭiṉ mutirāppiṟavi yāyp payaṉaṟṟatāyamintatu. kirāmvel iṟantatē ‘uḷḷatum pōyiṟṟu noḷḷik kaṇṇā’ eṉṟapaṭi kirāmveliṉ vīṟāppāṉa koḷkimaṭṭumiṉṟi naṭuniliyāḷar koḷkikaḷ kūṭa maṟakkappaṭṭup paḻiya aracāṭciyē iraṇṭām cārls varukiyuṭaṉ mīṭṭum vantatu.
āṉāl piriṭṭiṣ makkaḷ pōrāṭṭam muṟṟilum vīṇpōkavilli eṉpati iraṇṭām cārlas āṭciyum iraṇṭām jēms āṭciyum kāṭṭiṉa. tam urimikkāka muṭiyaracar taliyiyē tuṇikkat tuṇinta nāṭṭiṉ makkaḷ mīṭṭum aṭimikaḷāka vāḻvar eṉṟu etirpārkka muṭiyātaṉṟō? attuṭaṉ aracurimip pōrkkālattil ēṟpaṭṭa “nīṇṭa araciyal maṉṟa”ttiṉ(ṭuṭipē ñayacaṭayianavē) muṟpakutiyil niṟivēṟiya naṭunilic cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ pala niliyāka niṉṟu āṅkila araciyalil iṭampeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ maṉṉar vari cumattum urimi muṟṟilum kaṭṭuppaṭṭatoṉṟu. maṉṟattik kūṭṭātu mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷukkumēl āṭcipurivatit taṭuppatu, oḻuṅkāṉa vaḻakkumaṉṟa ārāycciyiṉṟip perumakkaḷi oṟukka ṭiyūṭar aracar amitta viṇmīṉ maṇṭalamaṉṟam, uyarāṇimaṉṟam(ūṭiracava ṭika ḷuvayaca ūhyaaneca, ūṭiracava ṭika ḻipā ūṭiaaḷiḷiṭin) mutaliya oḻuṅku maṉṟaṅkaḷ akaṟṟappaṭṭatu ākiyavi vēṟu cila. mēlum iraṇṭām cārls, iraṇṭām jēms ākiyavar āṭcikaḷilēyē āḷurimiccaṭṭam mutaliya mutaṉmivāynta caṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭimuṟikku vantaṉa. āḷurimiccaṭṭam(ḻayaṭaraḷa ūṭicayīraḷa huva) kuṟṟattāḷ vaḻaṅkātu oruvaric ciṟippaṭuttum urimiyik kaṭṭuppaṭuttiṟṟu. ciṟippaṭṭavar evarum iccaṭṭappaṭi taṉṉi vaḻakkumaṉṟattil koṇṭu vantu taṉ kuṟṟatti ārāyumpaṭi aracāṅkattukku muṟiyīṭu ceytukoḷḷalām. iṉṟaḷavum taṉimaṉitar urimiyik koṭuṅkōṉmiyiṭamiruntu kāppatil itu oppaṟṟa caṭṭamāyiruntu varukiṟatu. piṟa nāṭukaḷum ivvariya caṭṭatti mēṟkoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum aracurimi vilakkuccaṭṭappakarppu(nuouṭanaḷiṭin kṣaṭiṭa) oṉṟum koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. itu niṟivēṟiṉāl iraṇṭām cārlsukkup piṉ avar uṭaṉpiṟantār jēms (iraṇṭām jēms) piṉṉāl aracarāyirukka muṭiyātu. eṉavē uṭaṉpiṟantār urimiyik kākka maṉṉar atu niṟivēṟumuṉ araciyal maṉṟik kalittār. atik kaṭṭāyam niṟivēṟṟumpaṭi cilarum, niṟivēṟṟakkūṭāteṉṟu cilarum maṉṉariṭam maṉucceytaṉar. ivarkaḷē nāḷaṭivil vikkukaḷ(ṟuhpiḷa) ṭōrikaḷ(kūṭicaniḷa) eṉṟa irukaṭcikaḷāyiṉar. ivarkaḷuḷ vikkukaḷ sṭiyūvarṭ aracarkaḷi etirtta araciyalmaṉṟa muṟpōkkuk kaṭciyiṉar. ivarkaḷē iṟutiyil kattōlikkarāṉa jēmsit turatti avar marukaṉum puroṭṭasṭaṇṭu camayattiṉarāṉa mūṉṟām villiyatti aracaṉāka varavēṟṟavarkaḷ. ṭōrikaḷ sṭūvarṭṭukaḷi ātarittaṉar. mūṉṟām villiyam varuvati ivarkaḷ virumpavilli. aracarāṉapiṉ vēṇṭā veṟuppāy ēṟṟaṉar. piṟkālattil ivviru kaṭciyiṉarumē tammarapu māṟātu muṟiyē potuppaṭic cīrtiruttam kōrupavarākavum paḻami pēṇupavarākavum(ṭuinecayaṭaḷa, ūṭiḷēnaceyavienaḷa) māṟiṉar.
iraṇṭām jēms aracarkku etirāka vikkukaḷ ceyta kiḷarcci veṟṟiyaṭintatē, mutalām jēms mutalāka iraṇṭām jēms īṟāka sṭiyūvarṭ aracarkaḷ koṇṭa aracar urimikaḷ ellām yātoru cantaṭiyumiṉṟi otuṅkippōyiṉa. putiya aracaṉ āṭciyil veṟṟipeṟṟa araciyal maṉṟa muṟpōkkuk kaṭciyiṉarāka vikkukaḷ ammaṉṟiṉ niliyi mēlum uṟutippaṭutti aracaṉ valimiyi variyaṟukkum caṭṭaṅkaḷ ceytaṉar. piṟanāṭukaḷil peruṅkiḷarcciyuṭaṉum kuruti cintiyum naṭanta perumāṟutal iṅṅaṉam eḷitil niṟivēṟiyatu kaṇṭu varalāṟṟaṟiñar itaṉik kurutiyillāp puraṭci eṉṟum, pukaḻmikka puraṭciyeṉṟum cantaṭiyaṟṟa puraṭci eṉṟum pukaḻvārāyiṉar.
2. muyaṟcik kālam
āṅkilamakkaḷ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākak kiḷarcci ceytum pōrāṭiyum kiṭikkāta allatu kiṭittum kāla mutircci yiṉmiyālō aracaṉ āṟṟalmikutiyālō payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷa muṭiyāta urimikaḷ mēṟpōkkākap pārttāl arumuyaṟci etuvumillāmalē iraṇṭām jēms maṉṉar vīḻcciyiṉ piṉ putiya aracar āṭciyil kiṭittaṉa. āṉāl, uṇmiyil avi ōraḷavu mutal cārls aracaṉ āṭciyil ēṟpaṭṭa pōrāṭṭattiṉ tolippayaṉkaḷēyākum. kirāmvel āṭci maṭṭaṟṟa muṉṉēṟṟattik koṇṭuvara muyaṉṟataṉāl tōlviyaṭinta piṉ, iraṇṭām cārls aracar āṭciyuṭaṉ pala vakiyil paḻiya mutal cārls aracar āṭcimuṟiyē mīṇṭum naṭimuṟikku vantatu pōlat tōṉṟiṟṟu. āṉāl., oru taṭavi veṟṟiyuṭaṉ pōrāṭiya makkaḷ taṅkaḷ āṟṟali uṇarntu koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. iraṇṭām jēmsiṉ aṭakkumuṟi āṭciyi oru kaṭciyārē etirttaṉarāyiṉum nāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ perumpāṉmi ātaravu avarkaḷ pakkamiruntatu. eṉavē, jēms eḷitil aracirukki yiliruntu akaṟṟappaṭṭu avar putalviyāṉa mēriyum marukarāṉa mūṉṟām villiyamum aracarāyiṉar. veṟṟiyuṭaṉ maṉṉarit tērnteṭutta makkaḷ eḷitākat tam urimikaḷi nilināṭṭavum maṉṉarurimikaḷi variyaṟukkavum caṭṭaṅkaḷiyaṟṟiṉar. iṉṟiya āṅkila nāṭṭaraciyalamippil iccaṭṭaṅkaḷ talimiyāṉa iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ic caṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ urimip pakarppu(kṣaṭiṭa ṭika cuipāvaḷa), kiḷarccic caṭṭam(āravipē huva), aracar varuvāyc caṭṭam mutaliyavi taliciṟantavi. ivaṟṟuḷ urimip pakarppu mutal cārls aracar kālattil niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭa urimik kōrikkiyiṉ(ñanavaviiṭin ṭika cuipāvaḷa) putiya uruvēyākum. itil mutal cārls, iraṇṭām jēms mutaliya taṉṉāṇmimikka aracarkaḷ āṭcimuṟikaḷum akkāla varimuṟikaḷum kaṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. urimip pakarppi maṉṉar tam āṟṟalāl mīṟi naṭantaṉar. āṉāl ‘kurutiyillāp puraṭci’yiṉ piṉ aṅṅaṉam aracar mīṟātapaṭi kākka vēṟu caṭṭaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ ciṟantaviyē kiḷarcciccaṭṭamum maṉṉar varuvāyc caṭṭamum ākum. kiḷarccic caṭṭam nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭum kiḷarccikaḷi aṭakkumpaṭi oru nilavarappaṭi vittuk koḷḷum urimiyi aracaṉukku aḷittatu. āṉāl, avvurimi nilipeṟṟa urimiyāka aḷikkappaṭavilli. atu cila āṇṭaḷavukkē urimiyaḷippatāyiruntatu. ākavē aracaṉ maṉṟatti aṭikkaṭi kūṭṭāmal paṭivaliyuṭaṉ āḷal muṭiyātu. itaṉuṭaṉ maṉṉar varuvāy vakiyilum māṟutal ceyyappaṭṭatu. itaṟku muṉṉellām ovvōr aracar āṭcittoṭakkattilum avar vāḻnāḷ muḻumikkum avar varumāṉam variyaṟi ceyyappaṭṭuc caṭṭam iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl araciyal maṉṟatti āṭcittoṭakkattil maṭṭum kūṭṭippiṉ kūṭṭāmalē avarkaḷāl āḷa muṭintatu. āṉāl ippōtō celaviṉ oru ciṟu pakuti maṭṭumē vāḻnāḷ muḻumikkum nilavara urimiyāka aḷikkappaṭṭatu. mīnta perumpakutiyum āṇṭutōṟum putuppikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu aracar varumāṉac caṭṭam variyaṟuttatu.
mēlum urimip pakarppiṉpaṭi aracaṉ kattōlikkaṉā yirukkavō kattōlikka māti maṇam ceytavaṉāka irukkavō kūṭātu eṉṟum variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu. ivvariyaṟi iṉṟum naṭi muṟiyilirukkiṟatu. perumpāṉmi purōṭṭas ṭāṇṭukaḷ (cīrtiruttac camayac cārpāṉavarkaḷ) āṉa iṅkilāntu makkaḷiṉ camayurimi iṅṅaṉam kākkappaṭiṉum kuṭikaḷip paṟṟiyavari paṭippaṭiyākap paḻiya camayakkaṭṭup pāṭukaḷ vilakkappaṭalāyiṉa. villiyam āṭciyilēyē muḻuk kattōlikkarkaḷum muḻumuṉṉēṟṟak karuttuṭiya purōṭṭasṭāṇṭukaḷum nīṅkalākap piṟarukkuc camayavāḻvil taṉṉāṇmi tarum camaya oppuravuc caṭṭam(kūṭiṭanacayaviṭin huva) oṉṟu niṟivēṟiṟṟu. paṭippaṭiyākap piṉṉāṭkaḷil muṟpōkku mikka purōṭṭasṭāṇṭukaḷ kattōlikkar yūtar ākiyavarkaḷukkum oppuravu urimikaḷ tarappaṭṭaṉa.
āṅkila araciyal vaḷarcciyil maṉṉar urimikaḷiṉ variyaṟiyiviṭak kūṭac ciṟappuṭiya oru perunikaḻcci villiyam āṭciyil toṭakkamuṟṟatu. atuvē āṅkilak kaṭci araciyal muṟiyākum. āṅkilanāṭṭu varalāṟṟil araciyalurimippōrk kālattiltāṉ nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē teḷivāṉa kaṭcippākupāṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu. ivi laṅkāsṭiriyar āṭcik kālattil ēṟpaṭṭa maṉṉar kuṭiyiṉariṉ aracurimik kāṉa pōrāṭṭak kaṭcikaḷ pōṉṟavi alla; kuṭimakkaḷ araciyal urimi mēmpaṭṭatā? maṉṉat araciyal urimi mēmpaṭṭatā? eṉpatip paṟṟi eḻunta kaṭcikaḷ ākum. iraṇṭām cārls maṉṉari mīṭṭum aḻitta pōtu ōraḷavu ikkaṭci vēṟṟumi ōyvuṟṟatupōl kāṇappaṭṭatu. āṉāl aracaṉ tampiyākiya iraṇṭām jēms aracurimiyi vilakkavēṇṭum eṉṟa vēṇṭukōḷ eḻuntapōtu mīṇṭum akkaṭci putturuppeṟṟatu. avvēṇṭukōḷik koṇṭu vanta kaṭci paḻiya maṉṉar kaṭciyiyum ataṉi etirtta kaṭci paḻiya araciyal maṉṟak kaṭciyiyum niṉivūṭṭum. iraṇṭām jēms aracirukkiyiliruntu nīkkappaṭṭapiṉ muṉ avari ātaritta kaṭciyilkūṭa oru cilarē avariṭam paṟṟuṭiyavarā yiruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum vaṭa skātlāntiluḷḷa mēṭṭunila makkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ piṟkālattil aikkōpiṭkaḷ eṉṟa peyarāl araciyalik kaviḻkkum nāṭṭup pakivaruṭaṉ oppāka veṟukkappaṭṭa kuḻuviṉarāyiṉar. jēmsi ātaritta kaṭciyil perumpālōr avar aracurimiyi ātariyāmal ōraḷavu avar koḷkiyi maṭṭumē mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ ṭōrikaḷ eṉṟum villiyam aracarik koṇṭuvantavarkaḷ vikkukaḷ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
mūṉṟām villiyam aracar oru kaṭcic cārpākavē iṅkilāntiṉ aracurimiyi ēṟṟāreṉṟālum virivil kaṭcic cārpiliruntu viṭupaṭṭu nāṭṭup potuttalivaṉā yirukka eṇṇaṅkoṇṭār. ākavē, avar tammi mutalil ātaritta vikkuk kaṭciyiṉariṭiyiliruntu maṭṭumallāmal ṭōrik kaṭciyiṉiriṭiyiliruntum amiccarkaḷip poṟukki eṭukka muṟpaṭṭār. āṉāl teṟkum vaṭakkumā yirukkum iruvēṟu kaṭcikaḷic cārnta amiccarkaḷika vittukkoṇṭu avar eṉṉatāṉ ceytal muṭiyum? ākavē, avar tam mutaṟkaruttik kiviṭṭuviṭṭu oru kaṭciyiliruntē amiccarkaḷit tērnteṭukkalāṉār. amiccarkaḷ araciyal maṉṟil celvākkuṭiyavarkaḷallātavarā yiruntatāl araciyal maṉṟattiṉ ātaravu aracaṉukku illi. kurutiyillāp puraṭcikkup piṉ av ātaraviṉṟi aracaṉ taṉittāṭci purivatum iyaluvatilli. eṉavē, maṉṟattil perumpāṉmi ātaravu koṇṭa kaṭciyiliruntē amiccarit tērnteṭukkum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. ivvaḻakkam nāḷaṭivil vērūṉṟi nilipeṟṟup piriṭṭiṣ araciyaliṉ eḻutāc caṭṭaṅkaḷ allatu marapukaḷuḷ oṉṟākiviṭṭatu. āṅkila araciyalamippiṉ uyirnilip paṇpākak karutappaṭum amiccar kuḻu amippiṉ vaḷarcciyil ikkōṭpāṭu mutaṟpaṭiyāṉa vaḷarcci muṟiyik kuṟippatākum. 1696-il mūṉṟām villiyam amitta vik amiccar kuḻuvē āṅkila araciyal maṉṟa varalāṟṟil mutal āṭci urimi peṟṟa amiccar kuḻu eṉṉalām. āyiṉum iraṇṭām cārls aracar āṭcikkālattil ēṟpaṭṭa vik amiccar kuḻuvākiya jūṇṭōvi(turavēṭi) itaṟku oru muṉmātiri eṉṉalām.
amiccar kuḻuvamitiyiṉ iraṇṭāvatu vaḷarccippaṭi amiccar orumippāṭum kūṭṭuppoṟuppum ākum. iraṇṭām cārls aracar āṭciyilēyē amiccar oruvar karuttu vēṟupāṭuṭiyavarāṉāl kuḻu muḻumiyum kaliyum vaḻakkam iruntateṉiṉum atu iṉṟiyamiyā ēṟpāṭāka villi. uṇmiyil ataṉi iṉṟiyamiyā ēṟpāṭākkiya perumi mutalām jārj aracar kālattil mutal amiccarāyirunta vālpōl eṉṟa periyāratēyākum.
villiyam kālamutal cilacamayam vikkukaḷum cila camayam ṭōrikaḷum vaṉmiyuṭiyavarāyiṉar. villiyam āṭciyiṉ iṟutiyilum āṉ āṭciyilum perumpālum ṭōrikaḷē mīṇṭum talimi nilikku vantaṉar.
villiyam, mēri ākiyavar piḷḷiyaṟṟavarātalāl avarkaḷukkup piṉ mēriyiṉ taṅki āṉ araciyāṉāḷ. avaḷum piḷḷiyillātu pōyiṉaḷ. aracurimikku āṉukkup piṉ urimiyuṭiya pala aṇmi uṟaviṉar kattōlikkarā yiruntaṉar. caṭṭappaṭi kattōlikkar aracarāka muṭiyātāyiṉum eṅkē kiḷarcci valimiyāl aracāṭci avarkaḷ kiyil cikkiviṭumō eṉṟu añciya āṅkilamakkaḷ mikat tolivāṉa urimiyiyē yuṭiya haṉōvar mutaṉ makaṉāṉa jārjē(ḻunaṭicapana, nuṭanuvaṭica ṭika ḻayanṭienaca) aracaṉāka varavēṇṭum eṉac caṭṭam vakuttaṉar. ivar mutalām jēmciṉ putalviyāṉa elicapettu, avaḷ kaṇavaṉ pālaṭṭiṉ mutaṉ makaṉār piraṭerikākiyavariṉ makaḷ piḷḷiyāvar. tāyiṉtāy vaḻiyil maṭṭumē ivar āṅkila nāṭṭuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavar. inta hāṉōvar kuṭiyiṉar araciyal maṉṟattār viruppattāl aracarāyiṉum iraṇṭu tali muṟivari paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷilum pēccilum muṟṟilum jermāṉiyarākavē iruntaṉar. āṅkilamoḻi pēcavō āṅkilēyariṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyaṟiyavō ceyyāta ivarkaḷ perumpālum āṭcippoṟuppi muṟṟilum amiccarkaḷiṭamē viṭṭuviṭa nērntatu.
taṟceyalāka nērnta inta nilimi āṅkila araciyal vaḷarccikku utavum oru periya naṟpēṟāka muṭintatu. peyaraḷavilum eḻuttuvaṭivil caṭṭattiṉpaṭiyum amiccarkaḷ aracaṉāl amarvupeṟṟu aracaṉukkē iṉṟaḷavum poṟuppuṭiyavarāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, mutaliraṇṭu haṉōvariya aracar (mutalām iraṇṭām jārjukaḷ) kālamutal uṇmiyil avarkaḷ araciyal maṉṟattiṟkē poṟuppuṭiyavarkaḷākavum ataṉ viruppu veṟuppiṉālēyē amarvum nīkkamum peṟṟavarkaḷākavum āyiṉar. attuṭaṉ maṭṭumiṉṟi maṉṟattiṉ viruppam avarkaḷi orē kuḻuvāka aracaṉ ceyalukkup poṟuppuṭiya varkaḷākak koṇṭu niṉṟataṉāl avarkaḷiṭiyē kūṭṭuppoṟuppum vaḷaralāyiṟṟu. amiccar kuḻuvamitiyiṉ ivviru kūṟukaḷum iraṇṭām cārls aracar kālattilēyē vittūṉṟiyaviyāyiṉum villiyam āṉ ākiyavarkaḷ aracāṭciyil kurutiyillāp puraṭciyiṉ payaṉāka urampeṟṟu mutaliraṇṭu haṉōvariyariṉ āṭcikkālaṅkaḷil vērūṉṟi āṅkila araciyaliṉ uyirnili aṭippaṭiyāka nilipeṟṟaṉa.
itēcamayam amiccarkuḻuviṉ uṟuppiṉar potup pēraviyiṉiṉṟumē tērnteṭukkap paṭṭataṉālum; ataṟkē avarkaḷ poṟuppuṭiyavarāy ataṉ āṟṟalukkē kaṭṭuppaṭṭavarāyiruntataṉālum; aracar peyarāl amiccar āṭciyi naṭattuvataṟku uyirnili vēṇṭutalāṉa poruḷvaruvāyum vari pirittalum atē potuppēraviyiṉ kiyiliruntataṉālum potup pēraviyē nāṭṭiṉ potuppēraviyāka vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. perumakkaḷ avi itaṟkumāṟākap pala vakiyilum āṟṟalum urimikaḷum iḻantu vantatu. villiyam, āṉ mutaliyavarkaḷ āṭcik kālaṅkaḷil aracar, perumakkaḷ aviyil vaḷarcciyaṭintu varum kaṭcik ketirāka valivu tēṭiya kālattil putiya perumakkaḷi ākkavum muṟpaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl perumakkaḷ pēravi aracar kiyilum avvaracar peyarāl āṭci naṭattum naṭimuṟikkuḻuvākiya amiccar kuḻuviṉ kiyilum iṟutiyil avvamiccar kuḻuviṉi iyakkiya potu aviyiṉ kiyilum oru pommiyāka māṟi vantatu.
āḷ āṭcikku iraṇṭāṭcikaḷilum (1714 mutal 1761 vari ṭōrikaḷiṉ āṟṟalum pukaḻum maṅkiyiruntaṉa. vikkukaḷ mēlōṅki yiruntaṉar. toṭakkattil vikkukaḷiṉ talivarāy ṭuṉṣeṉṭ eṉpavarē mutalamiccarāyiruntār. āṉāl avar maṉṉaruṭaṉ iṇintu veḷināṭṭu araciyalilum pōrilum peritum īṭupaṭṭatuṭaṉ āṅkila makkaḷukku aiyamum kalavaramum uṇṭupaṇṇum vakiyil maṟivāṉa uṭampaṭikki kaḷilum kalantukoṇṭaṉar. poruḷamiccarāyirunta avar mittuṉar rāparṭ vālpōl veḷināṭṭup pōrkaḷi veṟuttavar. iṅkilāntu, airōppiya nāṭukaḷiṉ pūcalkaḷil kalakkāmal otuṅki niṉṟu amitiyuṭaṉiruntāltāṉ atu vāṇipattilum kittoḻililum ciṟantōṅka iṭamuṇṭu eṉṟu avar eṇṇiṉār. avar etirppāl 1717 mutalē vikkuk kaṭciyil piḷavu ēṟpaṭṭatu. 1721 il veḷināṭṭuk kāriyaṅkaḷilum uḷnāṭṭuc ceytikaḷilum makkaḷiṉ etirppukku ṭuṉṣeṇṭu āḷākavē avar cārpāṉa vikkuk kaṭci vīḻcciyuṟṟup putiya vikkuk kaṭciyiṉ talivarāy vālpōl mutalamiccar niliyēṟṟār.
ṭuṉṣeṉṭukku uḷ nāṭṭil etirppi uṇṭu paṇṇiya nikaḻcci ‘teṉkaṭaṟ kumiḻi’ eṉap piṟkālattil ēḷaṉamākak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa teṉkaṭal vāṇipak kaḻakattiṉ muṟivu ākum. ceṉṉiyil irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil potumakkaḷ ciṟuporuḷ tiraṇṭa peruṅkuviyi viḻuṅki ēppamiṭṭa arppatnāṭ kaḻakattip pōla itu āṅkila makkaḷil ellā vakuppiṉariṭiyēyum poruḷiḻappiyum tiṇṭāṭṭattiyum kuḻappattiyum uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu. vālpōl poruḷiyalaṟiñar, tiṟamiyāṉa āṭciyāḷar. ataṉōṭu avar annāḷiya niliyi naṉkaṟintavar. paḻaṅkāla nilakkiḻamiyiṉ vāḻvumuṟi nīṅki vāṇikak kittoḻil tuṟikaḷil aṟiviyal puraṭci ēṟpaṭuttum putiya ūḻi toṭakkamuṟṟatu. akkālamē paḻiya vāḻviṉ aḻiviliruntu putuvāḻvu piṟappataṟku muṟpaṭṭa iraṇṭupaṭṭa niliyil ēṟpaṭum vāḻviyal poruḷātārak kuḻappaṅkaḷaṉittiyum vālpōl tiṟampaṭa amittu oḻuṅkupaṭutti āṅkila makkaḷ ātaravip peṟṟār. attuṭaṉ araciyal naṭimuṟiyilum maṟṟa amiccarkaḷi orumip paṭutti amiccar kuḻuvāṭci muṟikkum naṉku aṭikōliṉār. iṅkilāntiṉ tiṟamikka mutal taliyamiccar ivarēyāvar.
kālattiṉ iyalpukkum kaṭcit talimiyiṉ vēṇṭutalukkum ēṟpa araciyal vallunar eṉṟa muṟiyil kikkūli, celvarkuḻuttalimi, cūḻccimuṟi ākiyavaṟṟi avar kiyāṇṭatuṇṭāyiṉum akkālam araciyal vāḻviṉ mutirā iḷamikkālamātaliṉ avi puṟakkaṇikkattakkavi. āṉāl, āṅkila araciyal poruḷiyal vāḻvukkum avaṟṟiṉ mūlam ulaka vāḻvukkum avar āṟṟiya paṇi, oppuyarvaṟṟa tākum.
oru nāṭṭiṉ vāḻvil pala tuṟikaḷilum ceyaṟkariya ceyyum periyār tōṉṟi makkaḷukkut talimi tāṅki naṭāttuvatuṇṭu. attakiya periyārkaḷuḷ iruvakiyiṉar uḷar. kālattiṟkēṟṟa talimi tāṅkiya periyār, kālattukku appāṟpaṭṭa talimittiṟaṉuṭiyār eṉa avarkaḷi vakuttuk kūṟalām. kirāmvelum vālpōlum kālattukkap pāṟpaṭṭa periyār eṉak kūṟavēṇṭum. kirāmvel kaṇṭa araciyal kaṉākkaḷi avar kālattu makkaḷ evarum kāṇavumilli; uṇarntu ottuḻikkavum muṭiyavilli. eṉavē, avi naṉavulakil tōlviyuṟṟaṉa. poruḷiyal tuṟiyil vālpōliṉ muṟikaḷum avvāṟē. avaṟṟiṉ uyarviyum naṟpayaṉkaḷiyum piṟkālattār pōṟṟiṉum kālattiṉ cūḻalukkuṭpaṭṭa kuṟukiya nōkkamuṭiya annāḷiya makkaḷ avaṟṟi etirttaṉar. etirppu mikutiyāka āka vālpōl taliminili naḻuvi etirikaḷiṭam ceṉṟatu. 1741-il avar araciyal niliyiṉiṉṟum nīṅki 1745-il ulaku nīttār. avarukkup potumakkaḷ etirppit tanta avaratu putiya cīrtiruttam iṟakkumati varikaḷi nīkki avaṟṟi uḷnāṭṭu varikaḷāka māṟṟiyatēyākum. itu mikacciṟanta muṟiyeṉap piṟkālattil ēṟkap paṭṭup piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatu.
haṉōvariya aracarkaḷil mūṉṟāvatu aracar mūṉṟām jārj eṉpavar. ivar mutaliraṇṭu aracarkaḷiṭamiruntu ellāvakiyilum māṟupaṭṭavar. ivar tantiyiyum pāṭṭaṉiyum pōl āṅkila nāṭṭāriṉ tāy moḻiyiyum paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum aṟiyātavarallar. nāṭṭumakkaḷuṭaṉ nāṭṭu makaṉāy avvirutuṟiyilum oṉṟupaṭṭavar. attuṭaṉ avar pēravāvuṭiyavarāṉatāl tam tantiyiyum pāṭṭaṉiyum pōl araciyal talimi muḻumiyiyum amiccar kuḻuviṉiṭamē viṭṭuviṭa oruppaṭavilli. sṭūvarṭ aracarkaḷ maṉattuṭkoṇṭa kaṉavukaḷiṉ cāyal avar maṉattilum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, avar araciyal maṉṟattiṉ ātaravināṭi atil celvākku mikka amiccarkaḷik koḷḷāmal, tam maṉattukkup piṭitta koḷkiyuṭivar kaḷiyē amiccarkaḷākat tērnteṭuttu avarkaḷutaviyālum araciyalāḷarkaḷiṭam annāḷil vaḻaṅkiya kikkūli, uyar paṇitaral mutaliya maṟivaḻakkaṅkaḷiṉ utaviyālum maṉṟatti vacappaṭutti aṭakkiyāḷa eṇṇiṉār. mūṉṟām villiyattiyum mutal jārjiyum koṇṭuvarak kāraṇarāyirunta muṟpōkku araciyal maṉṟak kaṭciyāṉa vikkukaḷiviṭa muṉ sṭūvarṭ aracarkaḷi ātarittavarkaḷiṉ kālvaḻivanta ṭōrikaḷē aracaṉiṉ ippōkkukku uṭantiyāyiruntaṉar. eṉavē, avar mīṇṭum anta ṭōrikaḷiṭiyē tam viruppattiṟku iṇaṅkupavarkaḷit talimi yamiccarākak koṇṭu āḷamuyaṉṟār. āṉāl, āṅkila araciyal vaḷarcci ittakiya nāṭakattukku iṭamtarum niliyiṉiṉṟu miku toli ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. varavara maṉṉarukkum maṉṉar kiyāṭṭikaḷāṉa amiccarkaḷukkum etirppu vaḷarntatu. ivaṟṟiṉiṭiyē amerikka viṭutalippōr eḻuntatu.
mūṉṟām jārj aracarum avar amiccarkaḷākiya nārt perumakkaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷum āṅkila nāṭṭilēyē viṭutalip paṟṟāḷarkaḷi etirttavarkaḷ. āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyal talimikkuk kīḻppaṭṭirunta amerikkak kuṭikaḷurimikaḷi avarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam ātarippar eṉṟu nām etirpārkka muṭiyum? āṉāl kālattiṉ māṟutal amerikkak kuṭiyēṟṟattāri viṭutaliyārvattāl valimippaṭutti oṟṟumippaṭuttiyatu.  jārj vāṣiṅṭaṉ eṉṟa oppaṟṟa talivariṉ kīḻ avarkaḷ tam araciyal kiḷarcciyi oḻuṅkāṉa paṭittiṟa etirppākki viṭutalip pōrceytu veṟṟipeṟṟaṉar. amerikka nāṭṭiṉar viṭutali, aḵtāvatu āṅkila nāṭṭār amerikka nāṭṭiṉmītu celuttiya āṭciyi iḻantami āṅkila makkaḷit taṭṭiyeḻuppiṟṟu. tam aracar viṭutalikku etirimaṭṭumallar; tiṟamumaṟṟavar eṉṟu atu avarkaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭiṟṟu. mūṉṟām jārjiṉ taṉṉāṇmi āṭcikkum avar cārpāḷarāṉa nārtperumakaṉ amiccar nilikkum itu cāvumaṇi yāyiṟṟu.
amerikkak kiḷarcciyiṉ pōtu amerikkak kuṭimakkaḷ kōrikkiyi ātarittu niṉṟa āṅkilap peruntalivar iruvar. oruvar cātam kōmakaṉāṉa (mūtta) villiyam piṭ eṉpavar. iṉṉoruvar āṅkilanāṭṭiṉ oppuyavarvaṟṟa coṟpoḻivāḷarāṉa pak eṉpavar. ivarkaḷil muṉṉavari aracar mutal amiccarākka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉar. āṉāl, avar aracarip pōla amerikkari etirkkavum virumpāmal vikkukaḷip pōl avarkaḷ viṭutaliyiyum ēṟṟukkoḷḷāmal avarkaḷi mīṭṭum āṅkilap pēraracil cērkka virumpiṉatāl paṇiyēṟka muṭiyavilli. ataṉōṭu ciṉṉāṭkaḷukkuḷ avar uyirnīttum viṭṭaṉar.
amerikka viṭutalippōr āṅkilap pēraraci orupuṟam ōraḷavu kalippatāyiruntālum iṉṉorupuṟam ataṉi vēṟupakkam tiruppip puttuyir taruvatāyiruntatu. amerikka nilattil āṅkilappaṭikaḷ tōṟṟapiṉ iṉippēraraciṉ kaṭalkaṭanta pakutikaḷi viṭātirukka vēṇṭumāṉāl kaṭalāṟṟal mikutiyāka vēṇṭumeṉṟum kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷiṭam kuṟukiya taṉṉala maṉappāṉmiyi muntuṟak kāṭṭal kūṭāteṉṟum āṅkila makkaḷ uṇarntaṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉāka āṅkila nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟpaṭi vaṉmipeṟa virivupaṭuttappaṭṭatu. amerikkak kūṭṭuṟavu nāṭṭiṉ(runvinaṉa ḷuvayavanaḷa ṭika haanaciuya) iḻappiṟ kīṭāka virivil ataṉ vaṭakkil kāṉaṭā piriṭṭiṣ āṭcikkuḷ oṉṟupaṭuttappaṭṭatu. intiyāvilum piriṭṭiṣ ki mēlōṅkip piriṭṭiṣ āṭci virivupaṭat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āstirēliyā, niyūcilāntu, teṉ āppirikkā ākiya kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷ putuvatākat tōṉṟiṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ piriṭṭaṉ mūṉṟām jārjiṉ koḷkikaḷi mīṭṭum piṉpaṟṟātu eccarikiyāka naṭantu koṇṭatu.
āṉāl, eppaṭiyum amerikka viṭutalip pōriṉ atircci āṅkilap pēraraci maṭṭumaṉṟi ulaka varalāṟṟiyē mikavum tākkum ceyalāka nilavātirukka muṭiyavilli. amerikkarik kaṇṭu iṅkilāntiṉ araciyaliṉ kīḻt tuyaruṟṟa ayarlāntu makkaḷ tāmum viṭutalipeṟa vitir vitirttaṉar. amerikka makkaḷiṉ viṭutaliyiyakkattiṉ pōtu eṭuttāṇṭa putiya karuttukkaḷ kaṭuṅkōṉmiyilum uyarvakuppāriṉ curaṇṭalilum koṭumiyilum kiṭantuḻaṉṟa pirañcunāṭṭu ēḻimakkaḷ uḷḷattil kiṭantu kumuṟip palavakik kaṉākkaḷiyum ceyalārvattiyum tūṇṭiṉa. āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyaliyum meyviḷakka nūlkaḷiyum kaṟṟa pirañcup pēraṟiñar ikkaṉākkaḷukkuc ciṟaku tantu makkaḷ vāḻvu, araciyal, poruḷiyal ākiyavi paṟṟiya puttārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷuḷ rūcō eṉṟa pēraṟiñar eḻutiya vāḻviyal kuḻu oppantam(ūṭivēcayuva ḷuṭiuyiṭa) maṉita vakuppiyē taṭṭi eḻuppiya oru cila nūlkaḷil oṉṟāka iṭampeṟattakka tākum. itupōla iṉṉum vāltēr mutaliya piṟa aṟiñarum tam veṟitta muṟpōkkuk koḷkikaḷi makkaḷiṭiyē parappiṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉāka eḻunta pirañcup puraṭcit tī 1785 mutal pukintu 1792-il kaṉaṉṟu 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṭivil pērāravārattuṭaṉ airōppiya nāṭukaḷ aṉittum kiṭukiṭukkumpaṭi veṭittu nālāpakkamum kaṅkukaḷiyum veppattiyum parappiṟṟu. iṟutiyil ip puraṭciyāl kuḻappamuṟṟa pirāṉcu nāṭu neppōliyaṉ pērāvaluk kiriyāy, avaṉ kālaṭiyil paṭṭukkiṭantatu. avaṉukkup piṉ vanta aracarkaḷ āṅkila aracar utaviyuṭaṉum piṟa airōppiya aracar utaviyuṭaṉum perumakkaḷ ātaravuṭaṉum mīṭṭum ulakil kaṭuṅkōṉmiyik cilakālam nilināṭṭa muyaṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum immuyaṟcikaḷ muḻuvatum veṟṟi peṟātu vīḻcciyaṭintaṉa. ataṉ payaṉāka oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷil viṭutalippōrkaḷ, puraṭcikaḷ, nāṭṭurimi iyakkaṅkaḷ ākiyavi malintaṉa.
pirañcup puraṭciyiṉ ippērāravāram iṅkilāntiyum tākkāmalirukka villi. pirañcup puraṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa puraṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅku nilaviṉa. āyiṉum avaṟṟiṉ maṭṭaṟṟa muṟpōkkāl muṉ ōraḷavu muṟpōkkuṭiya talivarkaḷ kūṭa veruṭci yaṭintu piṉṉaṭiyalāyiṉar. ittakiyōruḷ amerikkap puraṭciyi ātarittavarum intiyāvil āṅkila āṭcit talivar vāraṉ hēsṭiṅsiṉ taṉṉāṇmip pōkki etirttavarum āṉa peruñ coṟ celvar parkkum oruvar. pirañcup puraṭciyiṉ veppamum vēkamum mikutiyāka āka, ittakiya nērniliyāḷarāṉa muṟpōkkāḷar palar avviyakkattiṉiṉṟum vilakiṉar. iṟutiyil neppōliyaṉ pēravāvāl puraṭci ceṉṟoṭuṅka ataṉ etirtākkuta liyakkamāṉa mūṉṟām neppōliyaṉiṉ vallāṭcimuṟi vantapiṉ eṅkum puraṭciyiyakkam aṭakkappaṭṭatu.
potuvil pūta uṭal iṟanta piṉṉarttāṉ pukaḻuṭal valivupeṟum eṉṟu kūṟuvatuṇṭu. pirañcup puraṭcivakiyil itu uṇmiyāyiṟṟu. veḷippaṭiyāṉa puraṭci oṭukkappaṭṭa piṉpē ataṉ uḷḷārnta uyirppaṇpukaḷiṉ āṟṟal veḷippaṭṭatu. ataṉ oḷipparappiṟpaṭṭa nāṭukaḷ aṉittumē periya māṟṟaṅkaḷ aṭintaṉa. av voḷi viḷakki mutalil ēṟṟiya nāṭākiya piriṭṭaṉ ataṉ tākkuk kuḷḷākāmaliruttal kūṭumō?airōppāviṉ tali nilattu makkaḷip pōla āṅkila makkaḷ uccanilikkaruttiyal ulakiṟ kutippavarallar āyiṉum, pirañcup puraṭciyirukka oḷiyik kaṇṭa avarkaḷ tam araciyal maṉṟa amippu, moḻittāḷ muṟi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ūḻalkaḷi muṉṉilum mikutiyākak kavaṉittu avaṟṟi ārvattuṭaṉ ārāyntu cīrtiruttam vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūkkuraliṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. toṭakkattil veṟiyāḷarāl ēṟpaṭṭa veruṭciyiṉ etirttākku nīṅkiyapiṉ muṟpōkkuṭiya talivar palar, ikkuṟi nīkka muṉvantaṉar. ataṉ payaṉākavē 1832-ām āṇṭu araciyal maṉṟac cīrtiruttamum ataṉip piṉpaṟṟiya maṟṟac cīrtiruttaṅkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. ivi varum piriviṟ kuṟikkappaṭum.
3. vaḷarccik kālam
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkam vari piriṭṭaṉiṉ araciyal maṉṟattiṉ urimikaḷ paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarntu urampeṟṟaṉavāyiṉum ammaṉṟa amippiṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa tērtalmuṟikaḷ mikavum cīrkeṭṭuk kuḻappaniliyilēyē iruntaṉa. māvaṭṭat tērtalil vākkāḷar takuti 1430 vari variyaṟukkappaṭṭāmalēyiruntatu. 1430-il vākkāḷar, 40 poṉ varuvāy matippuṭiyatākat tam urimiyil, allatu tam kivacamāka nilam uṭiyavarāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu variyaṟi ēṟpaṭṭatu. āyiṉum ivvariyaṟi teḷivāṉatāyilli. māvaṭṭa araciyal mutalvarum caṭṭa aṟiñarum virumpiyavāṟellām atip payaṉpaṭutta iṭamiruntatu. ittakiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ mutalil 37 iruntaṉa. nēraṭiyāka maṉṉarurimi uṭiyaviyāyirunta cesṭar, ṭarham ākiyavi neṭunāḷikkup piṉ tāṉ maṉṟattiṉ tērvil kalantaṉa. eṭṭām heṉriyiṉ kālattil vēlsnāṭum mutal jēms kālattil skāṭlāntum mūṉṟām jārji kālattil ayarlāntum araciyal maṉṟil iṭam peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ cārpāka skāṭlāntukku 48 pērum ayarlāntukku 100 pērum maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarāyiṉar.
nakarattokutit tērtalil 1832vari nilimi iṉṉum paṉmaṭaṅku kuḻappamāyiruntatu. tērtalurimi peṟṟa nakaraṅkaḷ toṭakkakkālaṅkaḷil teṉkiḻakkiluma teṉmēṟkilumē mikavum kuvintu kiṭantaṉa. vāṇikamum kittoḻilum vaḷarcciyaṭintu perukiya taṉ payaṉāka 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuḷ iṭināṭṭup pakutiyilum vaṭanāṭṭup pakutikaḷilum perunakaraṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl avaṟṟukkut tērvurimi illāmalēyēyiruntatu. pōka, aracarkaḷ tam viruppattukku ēṟṟapaṭi avvappōtu palavakip pōlit tērvut tokutikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. iviyum perumpālum kārṉvāl pōṉṟa teṉmēṟkup pakutiyilēyē amintaṉa. eṭṭām heṉriyiṉ kālattil iṅṅaṉam 38 putiya tokuti kaḷum elicapet araci kālattil 62 putiya tokutikaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉāy 1832-il oru puṟam makkaḷ nerukkamikka pala nakaraṅkaḷil tērtal tokutiyillāma liruntaṉa. iṉṉoru puṟam oru cila vīṭukaḷirunta iṭattiṟku iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku uṟuppiṉar tokutikaḷ vari tarappaṭṭiruntaṉa. muṉ ūriliruntu veṟum maliyākavō vayalākavō pōyviṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷilkūṭap pōlit tokutikaḷ iruntaṉa. avviṭattiṟku uriya perunilakkiḻavar avviṭattil tamakku vēṇṭiya āṭkaḷi moḻiyāḷarākat taruvittut tamakku vēṇṭiya āṭkaḷiyē tērnteṭuttukkoṇṭaṉar. pala tokutikaḷ celvarkaḷ caṭṭippiyiliruntu paṇam peṟṟa kiyāṭkaḷit tērnteṭukkum tokutikaḷākavē yiruntamiyāl avikaḷ ‘caṭṭippit tokutikaḷ’ eṉṟu ēḷaṉamāka aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pirañcup puraṭciyiṉ pēroḷi viḷakkam ittērtal ūḻalkaḷi makkaḷ ellōrum kavaṉikkumpaṭi ceytatu. mēl pirivil kuṟippiṭṭapaṭi 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ mutaṟ kāṟpakutiyil puraṭciyiṉ payaṉāka ēṟpaṭṭa veruṭciyiṉāl piṟpōkkāḷar ki, ciṟitu valuvaṭintatupōlat tōṉṟiṉālum ivveruṭci neṭunāḷ niṟkavilli. tērtal peyaraḷavil kuṭimakkaḷ tērvākat tōṉṟiṟṟēyaṉṟi atu uṇmiyil oru cila celvar kippaṭṭa āṭciyurimiyē eṉpatip potumakkaḷ naṉku uṇaralāyiṉar. rūsō, vālttēr ākiyavarkaḷi oppa iṅkilāntilum pentam eṉpavar urimi peṟṟa vakuppār āṭciyi āṇittaramākak kaṇṭittiruntār. puraṭci amaḷi aṭaṅkiyapiṉ jēms mil, pirāṉsis piṉēs pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ pentamip piṉpaṟṟik kuṭimakkaḷ urimi eppaṭi yirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati viḷakkip paḻiya kuḷaṟupaṭikaḷi mikavum kaṇṭittaṉar. nāṭṭuvāḻviṉ uyirnili nāṭiyeṉṟa nilikku vaḷarcciyaṭintuviṭṭa vāṇipak kittoḻil vakuppiṉarum tam urimiyip peṟa muṉvantu kiḷarcci ceytaṉar.
ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉākavē 1833-il araciyal cīrtiruttac caṭṭam ēṟpaṭṭatu. itu tērtal muṟiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyi māṟṟiṟṟu; tērtal tokutikaḷi mīṭṭum cīrtirutti amittut tērtal tokutiyiyum iṉṉum naṉṟāka variyaṟuttatu; mēlum moḻiyāḷar pativēṭu oṉṟu amikkappaṭṭatu. 2000 makkaḷukkuk kuṟivāka uḷḷa tokutikaḷ aṟavē oḻikkappaṭṭu 2000-ttukkum 4000-ttukkum iṭippaṭṭa makkaḷuṭiya tokutikaḷ ōr uṟuppiṉar tokutikaḷākak kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvakiyākap paḻiya cīrkēṭaṭinta nakarat tokutikaḷil 56 muṟṟilum talimuḻuk kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. 31 tokutikaḷil uṟuppiṉaruḷ ovvoruvar kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉar. ikkuṟipāṭṭiṉ mūlam eñciya iṭaṅkaḷ, māvaṭṭaṅkaḷukkum putiya pala nakaraṅkaḷukkumākap paṅkīṭu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. māñcesṭar, parmiṅhām ṣeḵpḵpīlṭu, līṭs ākiya pala nakaraṅkaḷ ivvakit tērtalil iṭampeṟṟaṉa. 10 poṉṉaḷavu pativurimi uṭiyavarkaḷum, 10 poṉ nīṇṭa tavaṇi īṭṭukkārarkaḷum, 50 poṉ kuṟukiya tavaṇi īṭṭukkārarkaḷum, 50 poṉ matippuṭiya iṭa āṭci yuṭiyavarkaḷum māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil moḻittakuti peṟṟaṉar. nakaraṅkaḷilum moḻittakuti orē nilippaṭuttappeṟṟatu. muṉṉellām tērtal kālaṅkaḷil tērtal takutiyuṭiyavar yār? takutiyaṟṟavar yār eṉpati māvaṭṭap paṇiyāḷarum caṭṭa aṟiñarumē vakutturittaṉar. ippōtu takutiyuṭiyavar peyarkaḷ pativu ceyyappaṭṭa piṉputāṉ avarkaḷ moḻi tara iṇakkam peṟṟaṉar.
ic cīrttiruttac caṭṭam muṉ irunta kuḻappa nilimiyi neṭuntoli tiruttiṟṟu. celvar kuḻuviṉ taṭaṅkalaṟṟa āṭci kuṟivu peṟṟatu. āṉāl muṟpōkkāḷar avākkaḷi atu muṟṟilum niṟivēṟṟavilli. oḻuṅku muṟiyiṉṟik kuvintu kiṭanta tērtal tokutikaḷ putiya nilikkut takkapaṭi caṟṟu oḻuṅku muṟikkuk koṇṭuvarap paṭṭaṉavē tavira orē niliyil tokutikaḷ amivu peṟavilli. makkaḷ toki viḻukkāṭṭiṉpaṭi tokutikaḷ amiyavumilli. atupōlac cila putiya tērtal takutikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉavē yaṉṟip paḻiya takuti muṟi akaṟṟappaṭavilli. muṉpu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil moḻi peṟṟavarkaḷ perunilakkiḻavar maṭṭumē. ippōtu attokuti caṟṟu virivuṟṟu vēḷāṇ makkaḷil mutaṉmiyuṭiyavaril cilariyum uṭpaṭuttik koṇṭatu. nakaraṅkaḷil perunilakkiḻavar, celvar ākiyōr kikkuṭpaṭṭirunta pōlit tokutikaḷil ippōtu vaḷarcciyuṟṟu muṉṉilikku vantu viṭṭa vāṇikak kuḻuviṉarākiya putiya iṭittara vakuppiṉarukkum moḻi urimi kiṭittatu. cīrtiruttac caṭṭattiṉāl iv vakuppiṉariṭiyē muṉṉilum 1,00,000 pērvari nakart tokutikaḷil moḻi urimi peṟṟaṉar. āṉāl, potumakkaḷ āṭciyurimi ivaṟṟiṉāl vantuviṭṭateṉṟu kūṟiviṭal muṭiyātu. ataṟkāṉa pōrāṭṭam iccīrtiruttac caṭṭattuṭaṉ toṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
cīrtiruttac caṭṭattiṉ payaṉkaḷuḷ tērtal muṟiyi viṭac ciṟantatu caṭṭa amippil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutalēyākum. ituvari caṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟu araciyal maṉṟattil niṟi vēṟiyaviyellām āṅkāṅku avvavviṭattukku vēṇṭiya cila naṭimuṟikaḷukkāṉa araciyal maṉṟa iṇakkamēyākum. avvan naṭi muṟikaḷ muṭivuṟṟapiṉ caṭṭam payaṉaṟṟaviyāy viṭum. āṭci muṟiyil niliyāṉa oḻuṅkukaḷi vakuttu ataṟkāṉa niliyaṅkaḷ amikkum putuvakic caṭṭaṅkaḷ 1832-ām āṇṭuc cīrtiruttac caṭṭattiṉ piṉṉālēyē ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. vaṟumi oḻippuccaṭṭam. nakaracapic caṭṭam ākiyavi ittiṟattavi. āṭcimuṟiyil akkaṟikoṇṭu ataṉāl naṉmikkum tīmikkum āḷāṉa nakarattokutikaḷiṉ iṭittara vakuppiṉarē ittakiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ niṟuvuvataṟkup peritum kāraṇarāyiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟalām.
iṅkilāntil cīrtiruttac caṭṭam ēṟpaṭṭa atē āṇṭukkuḷḷākavē aḵtē pōṉṟa caṭṭaṅkaḷ skāṭ lāntukkum ayarlāntukkum taṉittaṉi niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam orē aracil iṇintuviṭṭa mūṉṟu nāṭukaḷum oru niliyaṭintaṉa.
1832-ām āṇṭuc cīrtiruttac caṭṭatti aṭutta 1838-il nakart tokutikaḷiṉ tērtal urimiyil iṉṉoru māṟṟam ceyyappaṭṭatu. ituvari tērnteṭukkum urimi peṟṟirunta paḻiya nakar avi iṉṟiya niliyil nakaramakkaḷukkup poṟuppuṭiyatākat tirutti amikkap paṭṭatu. 1839-il piriṭṭaṉil maṭṭumaṉṟi piriṭṭiṣ pēraracu muḻumiyilum aṭimi vāṇikam oḻikkappaṭṭatu. av vāṇipattiṉāl perumpayaṉaṭintu ataṉ oḻippāl pēriḻappu aṭinta vaṇikarkaḷukku aracāṅkam 2 kōṭi poṉvari iḻappīṭākat tantatu.
pirañcuppuraṭci yiyakkattiṉ putteḻucci oṉṟu 1830-il pirāṉcu nāṭṭilum maṟṟa airōppiya nāṭukaḷāṉa jermaṉi, ittāli mutaliyavaṟṟilum eḻuntatu. ataṉ payaṉākak koṭuṅkōlaracarōṭu pēraracarkkum peruṅ kuṭiyāṭcit toṭarpuṭiya ciṟṟaracukaḷukkum etirppu ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. peljiya nāṭu hālantiṉiṭamiruntu viṭutali peṟṟut taṉiyāṭci niṟuviṟṟu. iṅkilāntil ivaṟṟiṉ etiroliyāka 1840-il muṉippāṉa kuṭiyāṭci yiyakka moṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu. ivviyakkattārkaḷ taṅkaḷ kōrikkikaḷi oru pattira uruvil vakuttuk kiḷarcci ceytataṉāl pattirakkārar (ūhyacavaḷiva) eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. ap pattiram muṉippāṉa kōrikkikaḷi vaṟpuṟuttiṉa. avi āṇṭu niṟivupeṟṟavar aṉivarkkum moḻi urimi, maṟivuṭiya moḻittaravu muṟi(kṣayaṭaṭaṭiva) āṇṭukku ōr araciyal maṉṟaṅ kūṭutal, caricamaviḻukkāṭuṭiya tērtal tokutikaḷ,tērtalil celvanilit takuti akaṟṟappaṭal, uṟuppiṉarkku ūtiyam taral ākiyavi. ik kaṭciyiṉariṉ kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa veṟitta pōkku pirañcup puraṭciyip pōṉṟē makkaḷukku veruṭci tantu muḻu veṟṟiyaṭiyāmal pōyiṟṟu. āyiṉum paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭuvataṟku atu tuṇi ceytatu. ayarlāntuk kuṭiyāṭci yiyakkattiyum itu valuppaṭuttiṟṟu.
vikṭōriyā araciyiṉuṭiya āṭciyiṉ naṭuppakutiyil iṅkilāntiṉ tali ciṟanta mutaṉ mantirikaḷum nāṭṭut talivarkaḷum āṉa piṭ, pāmarsṭaṉ, ṭisreyli ākiyavarkaḷ māṟi māṟi āṭcip poṟuppi ēṟṟu naṭattiṉar. kālap pōkkiṉ cuḻaliṟpaṭṭu avarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi taṅkaḷ koḷkiyi vaṟpuṟuttiyum kuṟittum kūṭṭiyum tiruttiyum vantaṉarātalāl paḻiya kaṭcip pākupāṭukaḷ palavakiyil māṟṟam peṟṟaṉa. nāṭṭil iṭittara vakuppār celvattilum celvākkilum paḻiya perunilak kiḻavariṉum mēmpaṭṭavarāy araciyalil valimi yuṭiyavarāyiṉar. muṉ avarkaḷ cārpil niṉṟu uḻittavarkaḷ liparal kaṭciyiṉarē yāyiṉum, ippōtu liparal kaṭciyiṉarukkup pōṭṭiyāka niṉṟu aṭikkaṭi kaṉsarvēṭṭiṅ kaṭciyiṉarum avarkaḷi ātarikka muṉ vantaṉar. liparal mantirikaḷ ceyya muṭiyāta cīrtiruttaṅkaḷic ceyyavum avarkaḷ arikuṟiyāka viṭṭavaṟṟi niṟivu paṭuttavum kaṉcarvēṭṭiv kaṭciyār muṉvaralāyiṉar. ittaṉi pōṭṭikaḷāl kaṭcikaḷ piḷavupaṭṭu aṭikkaṭi etu ekkaṭci eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyāta nilikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
āṅkila nāṭṭut talivarkaḷuḷ tuṇivumikkavarākiya ṭisrēyli iṭittara vakuppār, putiya toḻilāḷi vakuppiṉar ākiyavar uḷḷuṟak kontaḷippuṭiyarāki varuvati uṇarntu ‘veppattiṟkuc cariyāṉa māṟṟu veppamē’ eṉakkoṇṭu tam kaṭciyiṉarum etirkkaṭciyiṉarumē veruṭciyaṭiyat takka muṟpōkkuṭiya oru putiya cīrtiruttac caṭṭatti 1867-il koṇṭuvantār. itaṉpaṭi, nakart tokutikaḷ ettakiya uṭimi variyaṟiyum illāmal vīṭu uṭiyavar yāvarukkum moḻiyurimi tarappaṭṭatu. āyiṉum ayarlāntil maṭṭum 4 poṉvari variyaṟi ivvakiyil ceyyappaṭṭatu. taṟkālikamāka vīṭukaḷil taṅkupavarkaḷ kūṭa 100 poṉ vāṭaki koṭuppavarkaḷāṉāl moḻikku uriyavarāyiṉar. māvaṭṭap puṟaṅkaḷil āṇṭukkup 12 poṉ vāṭaki celuttuvōrkaḷ aṉivarum moḻi urimi peṟṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam 1832-ām āṇṭuc caṭṭam nakariṉ iṭittara vakuppārukkut tanta urimikaḷi 1867-ām āṇṭuc caṭṭam, nakariṉ uḻippu vakuppiṉarkkum tantatu. iṅṅaṉam araciyalil toḻilāḷar iṭam peṟṟataṉ viḷivākavē itaṟkup piṟpaṭṭa caṭṭa amippukaḷiṟ palavum toḻiṟ caṅkam niṟuvutal mutalil toḻilāḷarkkāṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷukku aṭikōlupaviyā yiruntaṉa.
1872-il pattirakkārar kēṭṭa maṟivāṉa moḻittara vurimi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ic caṭṭattiṟku mikak kaṭumiyāṉa etirppu iruntatu.
eṉavē, atu nilavaramāṉa caṭṭamāka niṟivēṟṟap paṭāmal, taṟkālikat tērvumuṟiyāka ōrāṇṭaḷavukku maṭṭum cellupaṭiyākumpaṭi niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. āṇṭutōṟum itē vakiyil itu neṭunāḷ putuppikkappaṭṭuvantu iṉṉum appaṭiyē vaḻakkappaṭi putuppikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. araciyal muṟiyil caṭṭamēṟpaṭuttāmalēyē ellārum ēṟṟu naṭattum naṭimuṟikaḷ pala āṅkila araciyal amippil vaḻakkaṅkaḷākavum caṭaṅkukaḷākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟi eḻutāccaṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟu puṉinturippatuṇṭu. āṇṭu tōṟum ic caṭṭam putuppikkappaṭuvatum attakiya oru naṭimuṟic caṭaṅkāy viṭṭatu.
araciyalamippuc cīrtiruttattil aṭuttapaṭi 1884-il niṟivēṟiya makkaḷ peyarāṇmic caṭṭamum(cunayīcanaḷanavēyaviṭin ṭika vāna ñanaṭiyīṭana huva) 1885-il niṟivēṟiya tērtal tokuti oḻuṅkuc caṭṭamum(cunaṉaḷivacireviṭin ṭika ḷunayavaḷa huva) ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ mutalatu, 1867-ām āṇṭuc caṭṭam, nakarttokutiyil vīṭuṭiyavarkku aḷitta atē paranta urimiyi māvaṭṭattukkum aḷittatu. atupōlavē, piṉṉatākiya 1885-ām āṇṭuc cīrtiruttac caṭṭam 1867-il nakarttokutiyil toḻilāḷar peṟṟa urimiyi nāṭṭuppuṟattut toḻilāḷarum aṭiyumpaṭi ceytatu. niluṭimi vīṭṭu uṭimikaḷ aṟṟa piṟavakip paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkum itaṉ mūlam moḻiyurimi kiṭittatu. aḵtōṭu araciyal maṉṟattiṉ ovvōr uṟuppiṉarkkāṉa tokutikkum moḻi tarum makkaḷ toki viḻukkāṭu orē niliyāka iccaṭṭattāltāṉ mutalmutal oḻuṅkupaṭuttap paṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉamākap pattirakkārar kōrikkikaḷuḷ maṟṟoṉṟu niṟivēṟṟappeṟṟatu.
araciyal maṉṟiṉ uṟuppiṉar toki ippōtu 658liruntu 670 āka uyarntatu. tērtal tokutikaḷ oru nilippaṭuttap paṭṭatu mutal uṟuppiṉarkaḷ avvat tokutikkē peyarāṭkaḷ āvar eṉṟa karuttup paṭippaṭiyāka māṟutalaṭintu avarkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ peyarāṭkaḷē eṉṟa karuttu mutirvuṟṟatu. uṇmiyil 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntē park eṉṟa aṟiñar itaṉi valiyuṟuttiyirunta pōtilum, itu naṭimuṟiyil naṉku varavēṟkappaṭṭatu. 1885-ām āṇṭuc caṭṭattiṉ piṉṉarēyākum.
araciyal maṉṟiluḷḷa iraṇṭu avikaḷuḷ kīḻavi potumakkaḷ avi eṉṟu neṭunāḷ vaḻaṅkiṉālum, uṇmiyil atu perumakkaḷ eṉṟa marapurimip paṭṭam peṟāta perunilakkiḻavar, celvar, ākiyavarkaḷ talimi urimipeṟṟa aviyākavē 1832vari yiruntu vantatu. 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu iṟutiyil ēṟpaṭṭa kittoḻiṟ puraṭciyālum, piriṭṭaṉiṉ pēraracum vāṇikamum vaḷarcciyaṭintataṉālum vaṇika makkaḷum, iṭittara vakuppiṉarum toḻilāḷarum vaḷarcci yaṭintaṉar. ivvaḷarcci kīḻaviyiṉ taṉmiyil perumāṟutali uṇṭupaṇṇi ati varavara uṇmiyākavē potu aviyākki vantatu. nāṭṭu makkaḷ vāḻvu, tāḻvu vaḷarcci, karuttu vēṟṟumi ākiya vāḻkkic ceytikaḷ aṉittiyum ip potuavi kaṇṇāṭi pōl eṭuttuk kāṭṭi ataṟkuriya uṇmiyāṉa peyarāṇmiyuṭiya aviyāka viḷaṅkalāyiṟṟu. itaṟku nērmāṟākap perumakkaḷ avi, evvaki vaḷarcciyum aṭiyātiruntatu. aṭikkaṭi aracarkaḷ putiya perumakkaḷit tam cārpil amippataṉ mūlam, atu taṉi āṟṟalum nāṭṭup poṟuppum iḻantu vantatu. mēlum poruḷ vaḻaṅkuvatum. mantirikaḷ amippatuma avarkaḷi iyakkuvatum potuaviyākavē amintatu. eṉavē caṭṭappaṭi perumakkaḷiṉ aviyuṭaṉ kalantu ataṉ iṇakkam peṟṟē potuavi caṭṭamiyaṟṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉum, araciyal naṭimuṟiyil perumakkaḷ avi, potuavip pōkkilēyē ellāvaṟṟiyum viṭṭuviṭṭatu. 
1832-ām āṇṭu variyil iraṇṭu avikaḷilum āṟṟal celuttiya vakuppukaḷ kiṭṭattaṭṭa orē niliyuṭiyaviyā yiruntapaṭiyāl iv vaviyil piṇakku illātiruntatu. 1832mutal 1885 vari putiya iṭittara vakuppiṉar kippaṭṭu muṉṉēṟiya potu aviyuṭaṉ perumakkaḷ avi avvappōtu muraṇpaṭṭatu. āyiṉum atu muṟukātiruntatu. 1893-il mutaṉ mutalākap potuavi niṟivēṟṟiya ayarlāntuk kuṭiyāṭcip pakarppip perumakkaḷ avi maṟuttatu. aracāṅkattil talimi tāṅkiyavarkaḷ uṟutiyāl ippiṇakku muṟṟavilli. āṉāl 1909-il aracāṅkattār koṇṭu vanta putiya varavu celavut tiṭṭattiṉpaṭi varumāṉa vari varumāṉa mikutikkēṟpap paṭippaṭiyāka uyarttappaṭṭatu. potu aviyil perutta etirppu irunta pōtilum atu niṟivēṟiṟṟu. āṉāl, perumakkaḷ avi ati maṟukkavē aracāṅkam āttiramuṟṟu maṉṟattik kalittup potuttērtal naṭattiṟṟu. aṭutta cila āṇṭukaḷil aracāṅka ātaravuṭaṉ potuavi perumakkaḷ aviyiṉ immaṟuppurimiyi oḻikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kacci kaṭṭit pōrāṭiṟṟu. 1919-il oruvakiyāṉa iṭinilit tīrppu ēṟpaṭṭatu. potu aviyiṉ tiṭṭa metaṉiyum perumakkaḷ avi maṟuttalum atu potuaviyil mūṉṟu taṭavi toṭarntu niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭāl atu perumakkaḷ iṇakkam peṟāmalē caṭṭamāy viṭum eṉṟu variyaṟi ceyyappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭil maṉṟattiṉ kālelli 7 āṇṭiliruntu 5 āṇṭukaḷākak kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarukku āṇṭukku 400 poṉ ūtiyam taruvateṉṟum uṟutippaṭuttappaṭṭatu. 1912-il ayarlāntu iru avi kaḷuṭiya taṉi maṉṟam peṟṟup piriṭṭiṣ araciyalamippi liruntu vilaki niṉṟatu.
mutal ulakappōr muṭivil mātar moḻi urimip  pōrāṭṭattil muṉintu caliyā muyaṟciyuṭaṉ pōrāṭiṉar. 1918-il niṟivēṟiya cīrtiruttac caṭṭam peṇkaḷil 30 āṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa aṉivarukkum moḻi urimi tantatuṭaṉ vīṭuṭiyavarkaḷ vīṭṭil taṅkiyirukkavēṇṭum kālelliyik kuṟittum, kappalōṭṭikaḷ, pōrvīrarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷukku moḻiyurimi tantum pattirakkārar kēṭṭa makkaḷ muḻuyurimi peṟṟa ayarlāntil vaṭapakutiyiluḷḷa alsṭar moḻiyurimiyiṉ perum pakutiyip potu makkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiviṭṭatu. mutal ulakappōril peṇkaḷ āṟṟiya toṇṭiṉ payaṉākavē avarkaḷukku immoḻiyurimi peṟṟa ayarlāntil vaṭapakutiyiluḷḷa alsṭar māvaṭṭattuc cīrtiruttac camayattavarkaḷ(ñacaṭivanaḷavayavēḷa). kattōlikkarkaḷāṉa ayarlāntu makkaḷ āṭciyilirukka virumpāmal ataṉi etirttaṉar. ataṉ payaṉāy 1920-il ayarlāntu iraṇṭu aracukaḷāka ākkappaṭṭu iraṇṭu maṉṟaṅkaḷ amikkap peṟṟaṉa.
 1924-ām āṇṭuc caṭṭam 30 āṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa āṅkila nāṭṭu āṭavar peṇṭir aṉivarukkum (uṟuppuk kuṟi yuṭiyavar, pittar, kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ ākiyavarkaḷ nīṅkalāka) moḻiyurimi tantatu. ittuṭaṉ 1848-il pattirakkārar kōriya muṉippu mikka cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ aṉittumē perumpālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟalām.
iṉṟu piriṭṭiṣ araciyal maṉṟiṉ potu avikiṭṭat taṭṭa 600 uṟuppiṉarkaḷi yuṭiya pēraviyākum. atil 30 pēr vels nāṭṭup peyarāṭkaḷ; 72 skāṭlāntiṉ peyarāṭkaḷ. ayarlāntu uṭpaṭṭiruntapōtu ataṉ cārpil 103 uṟuppiṉar iruntaṉar. perumakkaḷ aviyilum 620 uṟuppiṉar uṇṭu. atil araca kuṭumpattuk kōmakkaḷ iḷavaracikaḷ(ṉuramanaḷa ṉuruhnaḷaḷanaḷa) kōmāṉkaḷ(āyacaḷūranaḷaḷanaḷa) iḷaṅkōkkaḷ(nuyacaṭaḷa ēḷiuṭiravēḷa) perumakkaḷ(kṣayacaṭiḷē) vaḻakku maṉṟat talivarāṉa niṟi vāḻnāṭ perumakkaḷ(ṭukina yīnanacaḷa) talimakkaḷ(kṣiḷāṭiyīḷa) ākiyavar iruppar. talimakkaḷ mūṉṟām eṭvarṭu kālattil mikuti. āṉāl, talip perumakkaḷ iruvar(hacuhbiḷāṭiyīḷa ṭika laṭiravā yaṉē uyavēnacarecala) nīṅkalāka ippōtu 24 talimakkaḷē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.
toṭakkattil aracaruṭaṉ oppāyirunta perumakkaḷ valimi peṟṟiruntaṉar. perumakkaḷ aviyum aracaruṭaṉ vīṟṟiruntu tīrppaḷikkum mutaṟ peru maṉṟamāyiruntatu. iṉṟum muṭivāṉa mēlvaḻakkukaḷ ataṉ peyarālō ataṉ kiḷikkuḻu maṉṟaṅkaḷālō tāṉ naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. potuavi aracu vīṟṟiruntu āṇi ceyyum aracaṉ muṉṉum perumakkaḷ muṉṉum niṉṟu koṇṭu maṉṟāṭum aviyākavē toṭaṅkiṟṟu. iṉṟum aracar muṭiyēṟpu mutaliya taṟuvāykaḷil veḷiccaṭaṅku muṟiyil ippaḻakkam toṭarntu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. āṉāl, maṉṉar celavukkup paṇantarum urimi kāraṇamākap paṭippaṭiyāyp potuavi valivu peṟṟu mēmpaṭṭatu. maṉṉar ceyalāṟṟuṅ kuḻuvāka ēṟpaṭṭa amiccaraviyip potu avi tērnteṭuttu avarkaḷi iyakkiyataṉ kāraṇamāy paṭippaṭiyākap potuaviyē uṇmiyil āṭci puriyum aracāṅkattiṉ talimi aviyākavum, caṭṭa aviyākavum muḻumutal muṟi maṉṟamākavum-curuṅkac coṉṉāl uṇmi araciyal maṉṟamākavum viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
mūṉṟām pirivil kuṟippiṭṭatu pōlavē iṉṟiya piriṭṭiṣ aracāṅka muṟi ippotuavi mūlam muḻup poṟuppu vāynta kuṭiyāṭci naṭattum kuṭiyaracē yāyiṉum, peyaraḷavil aracurimi yaṟikuṟiyākiya muṭiyaraciyum, perumakkaḷ āṭciyiṉ kuṟiyīṭākiya perumakkaḷ aviyiyum, kuḻuvāṭciyākiya amiccar kuḻuviyum, vallunar āṭciyāṉa niliyāṉa paṇiyāḷariyum koṇṭu tikaḻkiṟatu.
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakuti mutal araciyal maṉṟiṉ vāḻvil oru putiya nili ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṟatu. muṉṉellām varaṉmuṟiyāṉa iraṇṭu kaṭcikaḷ tām māṟi māṟit talimi nilikkup pōrāṭiṉa. kaṉsarvēṭṭivkaḷ perumpālum uyar kuṭimakkaḷ cārpilum, perum pēraracu nilic cārpilum, māṟṟam vēṇṭāp paḻami pēṇum cārpilum niṉṟaṉar. māṟṟam vēṇṭumeṉṟataṉōṭu caṟṟup paranta maṉappāṉmiyum pēraracu niliyil taḷarvum kōriyavarkaḷ liparalkaḷ. vikṭōriyā āṭciyiṉ iṟutiyiliruntu 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kittoḻila puraṭciyiṉ payaṉāy mutalāḷikaḷ, toḻilāḷikaḷ eṉṟa vakuppukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. mutalāḷikaḷ tam nilikkēṟpak kaṉsarvēṭṭiv kaṭciyi ātarittum toḻilāḷar cilakālam liparalkaḷuṭaṉ cērntu uḻittum vantaṉar. ciṟcila camayam innili māṟiyatum uṇṭu. āṉāl 1920kkup piṉ itu vaḷarcciyuṟṟu iv vakuppu taṉik kaṭciyāka vaḷampeṟṟatu. nāḷaṭivil maṟṟa iru kaṭcikaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉ tuṇikoṇṭu araciyalil talimi niliyi yaṭiyavum ceytatu. ulaka iraṇṭām pōriṟutiyil avarkaḷ taṉippaṭa niṉṟē aracāṅkamum amikka muṭintatu.
āṭcimuṟiyil piriṭṭaṉ muḻukkuṭiyāṭciniliyi eytiya pōtilum, toḻiṟ kaṭciyiṉar kaṇ koṇṭu, atilum ciṟappāka atil muṉitta muṉṉaṇiyiṉar kaṇ koṇṭu pārttāl vāḻviyal amippuk kāraṇamākac celva nili uyarvu tāḻvukaḷ pēṇappaṭṭu, uṇmic cari oppu nili illātirukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. jermaṉiyil tōṉṟiya ulakap pēraṟiñar mārks eṉpavar tōṟṟuvitta vāḻviyal oppu neṟi(ḷuṭiuyiṭaḷia) im muṉitta karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu vaḷarcci yaṭintu uruciya(curaḷaḷayi) nāṭṭil potu uṭimik kaṭciyākak kāyttup potu uṭimi aracāṅkamākak kaṉintuḷḷatu. iv uruciya nāṭu oṉṟu nīṅkalāka vēṟenta nāṭum piriṭṭaṉaḷavu taṉ muyaṟciyāl araciyal muṉṉēṟṟattil toṭarntu mēmpaṭṭatilli eṉpatik kāṇalām.
maṟṟa nāṭukaḷiṉ araciyal vaḷarcci muṟikaḷi varum pirivukaḷil kāṇpōm.
6. amerikkanāṭṭu araciyal vaḷarcci
amerikka nāṭṭu vāḻviyal paṇpukaḷum nākarikamum varalāṟṟu muṟippaṭi pārttāl piriṭṭaṉ vāḻvu nākarikam ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu piṟantu vaḷarnta kiḷikaḷēyākum. āṉāl an nākarikam muṟṟilum kiḷi nākarikam aṉṟu. amerikka nāṭṭil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ perumpālum āṅkila moḻi pēcupavarē yāyiṉum ciṟitum peritumāṉa pala tokippaṭṭa makkaḷ piṟa airōppiya nāṭukaḷiliruntum ceṉṟu aṅkē kuṭiyēṟi avarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantatuṇṭu. mēlum avarkaḷukku vaṭakkiluḷḷa kāṉaṭāk kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭṭil perumpālum pirañcu makkaḷē kuṭiyēṟi yiruntaṉar. teṟkil meksikkō, teṉ amerikka nāṭukaḷākiya piresil mutaliyavaṟṟilum perumpāṉmiyiṉarākak kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ spāṉiya nāṭṭiṉar āvar. iṉṉum teṟkuk kōṭiyil pala airōppiya nāṭṭiṉarum ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyum nāṭamittum iruntaṉar. āṅkilēyarum pireñcuk kārarum ittāliyarum jermāṉiyarum iṅkē pala vakiyil viraviyuḷḷaṉar.
mēlum palvēṟu nākarikappaṭikaḷiluḷḷa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷākiya cev intiyarum ip  peru nilapparappiliruntaṉar. avarkaḷ veḷḷiyar kuṭiyēṟṟattāl tam nili keṭṭut taḷarntu aḻintukoṇṭē variṉum aṇmiyil ōraḷavu aḻiviliruntu pātukākkappaṭṭum varukiṉṟaṉar. tavira, amerikkāvil eṅkum iyaṟki vaḷam ciṟappuṭiyatā yiruppiṉum aṅkuḷḷa veppam, catuppu nilaṅkaḷiṉ puḻukkam, kāṭukaḷiṉ koṭu nilikaḷ, kuḷircuram mutaliya nōykaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi etirttut tāṅkit tāmē uṭaluḻippi mēṟkoḷḷa veḷḷiyar virumpavilli. eṉavē, avvakikkāka avarkaḷ nīkrōvar mutaliya kaṟuppu makkaḷi aṭimikaḷākak koṇṭu ceṉṟu avarkaḷ mūlamē nāṭṭi vaḷappaṭutta lāyiṉar. ittakiya vēṟu vēṟu vakippaṭṭa makkaḷ kalavikaḷukkiṭiyil vāḻnta amerikkar nākarikamum vāḻkkip paṇpum taṉi vāḻvu vāḻnta āṅkilēyariṭamiruntu vēṟupaṭutal iyaṟkiyē yaṉṟō?
mēlum iṅkilāntiliruntum piṟa veḷḷiyar nāṭukaḷiliruntum amerikkā ceṉṟavarkaḷ annāṭukaḷil iṉpa vāḻvu vāḻnta uyarkuṭi makkaḷō celvarō allar. perumpālum avarkaḷ vaṟumik koṭumikkañci ōṭiyavarkaḷum 16-17ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ēṟpaṭṭirunta airōppiya araciyalāriṉ camayac cārpāṉa aṭakkumuṟi kaḷiliruntu tappi ōṭiyavarkaḷum pēravāvāl untap paṭṭup perum poruḷ tiraṭṭum ārvaṅ koṇṭu ceṉṟa ūkkamikka iḷiñarumē yāvar. eṉavē avarkaḷiṉ putiya vāḻvil ip poruḷārvamum camaya viṭutaliyārvamum vakuppu vēṟṟumikaḷukkup paṇiyāta camanili yārvamum kuṭikoṇṭiruntaṉa.
18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kittoḻil puraṭciyāl palvakip poṟikaḷiṉ mūlam poruḷkaḷiṉ perukkatti mikutippaṭuttiya piriṭṭaṉ ap poruḷkaḷi viṟkum viṟpaṉiyiṭamākat taṉkuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷi naṭattat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. appōtu amerikkak kuṭiyēṟṟap pakutikaḷ āṅkila āṭciyi mīṟi yeḻuntaṉa. ērum kalappiyum kikkoṇṭa uḻavarkaḷ tiṭumeṉa vāḷum tuppākkiyum kikkoṇṭa pōrvīrarāka māṟit tam nāṭṭu viṭutalikkāṉa paṭiyākat tiraṇṭaṉar. uḻavaruḷ uḻavarāyirunta jārj vāṣiṅṭaṉ eṉpavar avarkaḷit tiṟampaṭat payiṟṟuvittu āṅkilēyari etirttup pōr naṭatti veṟṟi kaṇṭaṉar.
paḻam perum nāṭukaḷiloṉṟum vallaracukaḷil oṉṟumākiya piriṭṭaṉi varalāṟṟil oru nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavē vaḷarcciyuṭiya tam nāṭu etirttu veṉṟatu eṉṟa puttārvam avarkaḷ uḷḷārnta paṇpukaḷi ūkki avarkaḷit tamakkeṉa ōr araciyaliyum vakukkumpaṭi tūṇṭiṟṟu. itaṟkēṟpa avarkaḷ mutal talivarāṉa vāṣiṅṭaṉ ariya paṭit talivarā yiruntatuṭaṉ amiyāmal ariya araciyal amippāḷarākavum māṟi 1787-il amerikka viṭutali viḷamparam eṉṟa pērurimit tāḷi veḷiṭṭār. amerikkā nāṭukaḷiṉ taṉip paṇpukaḷukkuk kēṭiṉṟi oṟṟumi kuliyāta oru taṉippaṭṭa araciyal muṟiyi avar atil vakuttu amerikkāviṉ mutal talivarāka ulakil perumpukaḻ peṟṟār.
amerikkā nāṭṭil āṅkilēyarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya kālam elicapet, mutal jēms ākiyavarkaḷ āṭcik kālam ākum. nām ēṟkaṉavē muṉ pirivukaḷil kuṟippiṭṭapaṭi āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyal vaḷarcci ataṟku neṭunāḷ muṉṉarē toṭaṅki muṉṉēṟṟa maṭintu vantatu. puttam putitāka araciyal muṟi vakukkat tuṇinta amerikkap putuk kuṭimakkaḷukku itu oru muṉmātiriyā yiruntateṉpatu etirpārkkat takka iyalpēyākum. uṇmiyil araciyalamippiṟkāṉa oḻuṅkut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ vakukka muṟpaṭṭa amerikka nāṭṭut tuṇit talivarāṉa tāmas jeppercaṉ(kūhṭiayaḷa tunakakanacaḷaṭin) eṉpavar āṅkila araciyal maṉṟa naṭavaṭikkip pativēṭukaḷiyē avvakiyil peritum piṉpaṟṟiṉār. āṅkila nāṭṭip pōṉṟē amerikkāvilum iraṇṭu avikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭatum āṅkila nāṭṭip pōṉṟē tērtal muṟikaḷ, caṭṭaṅkaḷ amikkum kuḻu, uṭkuḻu naṭi muṟikaḷ ākiyavikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭatum itaṉālēyē yākum. āyiṉum iv vēṟṟumi perumpālum mēṟpōkkāṉa oṟṟumikaḷēyākum. araciyalamippi uṉṉippākak kavaṉippavarkaḷukku oṟṟumikaḷiviṭa uḷḷārnta vēṟṟumikaḷi mikuti eṉpatu teriyavarum.
itaṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ pala uṇṭu. mēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa māṟupaṭṭa cūḻnilikaḷ, kālanilikaḷ ākiyavi avaṟṟuḷ cila. mēlum amerikkak kuṭiyēṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭa kālam āṅkila araciyal payiṟcip paruvattilēyirunta kālam. mutal cārls kālattu araciyalurimip pōrum mūṉṟām villiyam kālattup pukaḻmikka puraṭciyum ataṉpiṉ hāṉōvariyar āṭcik kālamutal toṭaṅkiya amiccar kuḻup poṟuppāṭci muṟiyum vaḷarccikkālac cīrtiruttaṅkaḷum aṉṟu ēṟpaṭavilli. eṉavē, āṅkila makkaḷ mutirā iḷaṅkaruttukkaḷiyē tāy nāṭṭuṭaṉ potuvāṉa varaṉmuṟic celvamākak koṇṭu amerikkar ataṉit tam iyalpukkum cūḻnilikkum oppat tamakkeṉat taṉivaḷarcci muṟikaḷōṭu mēṟkoṇṭaṉar eṉpatu teḷivu.
amerikka araciyaliṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷuḷ talimi vāynta ceyti amerikkanāṭu āṅkilanāṭṭilum mikap periya parappu uṭiyatāyiruntatu eṉpatē. atil āṅkila nāṭṭip pōṉṟu camaya araciyal vāḻvu paṇpukaḷil orumippāṭu illi. ataṟku māṟāka vēṟupāṭukaḷ mikutiyāyiruntaṉa. eṉavē, āṅkilanāṭṭu araciyal pōṉṟa taṉiyāṭci araciyal annāṭṭukku ēṟpuṭiyatāka illi. amerikka araciyalil ovvoru kuṭiyēṟṟamum taṟcārpuṭiya oru taṉi aracākit taṉittaṉit talivarum taṉittaṉi araciyal maṉṟum āṭci muṟiyum koṇṭatu. āṉāl, atēcamayam perunilapparappiṉ potukkāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkap potu araciyalum iṉṟiyamiyātatā yiruntatu. eṉavē, attaṉi araciyalkaḷum taṉippaṇpukaḷum keṭāta muṟiyil avaṟṟi uṭpaṭuttiya oru kūṭṭu araciyalmuṟi(kunaṉanacayaṭa ḷuvayavana) amikkappaṭṭatu.
amerikka nāṭṭu araciyal muṟiyil iṉṉoru vēṟupāṭum uṇṭu. araciyaliṉ kūṟukaḷākiya caṭṭa amippu, vaḻakku naṭimuṟi, araciyal naṭimuṟi ākiya mūṉṟum amerikka araciyalil vēṟuvēṟu taṉippaṭṭa pakutikaḷākat tāṟcārpuṭiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkilāntilum amerikkar kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭkaḷil kiṭṭattaṭṭa itē nilimitāṉ iruntatu. nītinaṭimuṟi iṉṟaḷavum iṅkilāntil perumpālum araciyal naṭimuṟippakutikkuk kaṭṭuppaṭātirukkiṟatāyiṉum attuṟiyilum araciyal maṉṟiṉ nili ellāvaṟṟukkum mēṟpaṭṭa urimi niliyē yākum. caṭṭa amippu, araciyal naṭimuṟi ākiyavi paṟṟiyō veṉil hāṉōvariyar āṭcik kālattil ēṟpaṭṭa amiccar kuḻu āṭciyil iraṇṭum perumpālum oṉṟu paṭṭup piṉṉikkoṇṭuviṭṭaṉa. āṉāl, amerikkāvil innili ēṟpaṭavilli. kāraṇam amerikkar araciyal amippu ēṟpaṭuttum kālattil iṅkilāntilum annili ēṟpaṭavilli eṉpatē. mēlum innili piṟkālattilum ēṟpaṭuvataṟkup periya taṭi oṉṟu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. immūṉṟu tuṟikaḷum taṉittaṉi oṉṟuṭaṉoṉṟu cārpaṟṟu iruppatē uyarvuṭiyatu eṉṟu māṇṭiskyū(āṭivēnaḷaḷūranira) eṉṟa aṟiñar kūṟiyiruntār. amerikkariṭiyē ikkaruttu vērūṉṟic celvākkup peṟṟatu. āṅkila āṭciyiṉiṉṟu viṭupaṭṭa taṟuvāyil taṉi maṉitaṉāṭci mītu makkaḷ maṉakkotippu mikutiyāyiruntatu. amerikkāvil taṉimaṉitar, evar kiyilum muḻu urimikaḷum pōyviṭāmal pātukākka innili utavum eṉa avarkaḷ karutiṉar.
iṅṅaṉamāka amerikkāviṉ araciyalil araciyal naṭimuṟit tuṟikkut talivar nāṭṭuttalivar(ñacanaḷaṉinavē); avar talimiyiṉ kīḻiruntu amiccarum araciyal paṇiyāḷarum āṭciyi naṭattiṉar. caṭṭam amikkum tuṟi kāṅkiras(ūṭipēcanaḷaḷa) eṉṟaḻikkappaṭum araciyal maṉṟiṉ iraṇṭu avikaḷiṉiṭamum avaṟṟiṉ avittalivar kiyilum amintiruntatu. caṭṭa naṭimuṟi kūṭṭuṟavu araciyaliṉ talimi, vaḻakku maṉṟat talivar kiyilum kūṭṭuṟavu vaḻakku maṉṟam, tuṇimaṉṟaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉiṭamum iruntatu. im mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ talivarkaḷum maṟṟak kūṟukaḷukkō talivarkaḷukkō kaṭṭuppaṭṭaviyāyirāmal taṟcārpum taṉṉāṇmiyum uṭiyaviyākavē yiruntaṉa. eṉavē, āṅkila nāṭṭip pōla nāṭṭut talivarākiya mutal amiccarō amiccar kuḻuvō amerikkāvil araciyal maṉṟukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭu ataṉiṭam poṟuppu vakikkavilli. oḻuṅku muṟippaṭi kūṭṭuṟavut talivar tērnteṭukkappaṭṭu avarukkuriya kāla ellivari avar āṭci ceyvati yārum taṭukka muṭiyātu. ak kālelli kaḻinta piṟakō, avar ulaku nītta poḻutōtāṉ aṭutta talivar tērnteṭukkap paṭalākum.
kūṭṭu araciyaliluḷḷa itē nilitāṉ taṉi araciyalilum ēṟpaṭṭatu. nāṭṭut talivar niliyil iruntavar taṉi araciyaliṉ talivar(ḻuṭienacanṭica) aṅkum perumpālum araciyal maṉṟil iraṇṭu avikaḷ iruntaṉa. nāḷaṭivil ellā aracukaḷilumē iraṇṭu avikaḷ amintaṉa.
mutalil iraṇṭu avikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭatu āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyal marapip piṉpaṟṟiyēyākum. āyiṉum avi taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil amerikka nalaṉkaḷukku ukantaviyāka māṟupaṭṭu amintaṉa. ciṟitum peritum āṉa palaṉ araciyalkaḷ cērnta kūṭṭu araciyal ēṟpaṭuttuvatil avarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭa oru cikkali ipputu amipput tīrttu vittatu. ovvōr araciyalum tāṉ ciṟiyatāyiṉum periya araciyalukkoppākap peyarāṇmi peṟṟāltāṉ pātukāppu nili irukkum eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭṭatu. atē camayam potu araciyalil makkaḷ tokikkēṟṟa peyarāṇmi iruppatum iṉṟiyamiyātateṉak karutappaṭṭatu. araciyal maṉṟil iraṇṭu avikaḷ iruntataṉāl iraṇṭu muṟikaḷiyum(ḻaṭiraḷana ṭika cunayīcanaḷanavēyavienaḷa) piṉpaṟṟa muṭintatu. araciyal maṉṟiṉ kīḻavi (peyarāḷar avi) makkaḷ tokutikkēṟṟa peyarāṇmi uṭiyatāyiruntatu. mēlaviyō(ḷunayēvana) araciyalkaḷ ovvoṉṟukkum orē nilip peyarāṇmi yuṭiyatāyiṟṟu. kūṭṭuṟavil paṭippaṭiyāka vantucērnta aracukaḷ 30. ovvoṉṟukkum 2 āṭpeyarkaḷ vītam mēlaviyil 60 uṟuppiṉar iruntaṉar.
piriṭṭaṉiṉ araciyal maṉṟiyum amerikkā araciyal maṉṟiyum pārppavarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaṭa amippilum uṟuppiṉar irukkum muṟiyilum naṭi muṟiyilum kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumikaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi. iṅkilāntiṉ avikkaṭṭaṭam uṟuppiṉar tokikaḷi nōkka mikac ciṟitu. eṉavē, iṭavamippuṅ kuṟivu. amiccarkaḷ, eḻuttāḷar, paṭiyāḷar tavira maṟṟavarkaḷ nerukkamāy varici variciyāka aṭukki aminta vicippalakikaḷil(kṣananuhnaḷa) tāṉ uṭkāntiruppār. ivikaḷ perumpālum aviyil naṭu iṭivaḻikku(ūṭicacaṉiṭica) irupuṟamum etiretirāka aṭukkappaṭṭirukkum. aracāṅkattil paṇiyēṟṟu naṭattum kaṭciyiṉar valappuṟamum avarkaḷi etirppavar iṭappuṟamum vīṟṟiruppar. ivaṟṟukkappāl kaṇkāṭcikkāka varum viruntiṉariṉ māṭippaṭikaḷ(ēḷiviṭicaḷa ḻuyaṭaṭanacala) oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. poruḷāṇmi irukki(kūcanayaḷaracala kṣananuh) eṉṟa talimiyiṭattilēyē tattam mēṭippalaki yuṭaṉ amiccarkaḷ iruppar. ellāvaṟṟiṟkum talimiyil tāḻnta mēṭip palakiyil maṉṟa eḻuttāḷarum avarukkup piṉ uyarnta mēṭip palakiyiṉ etiril avit talivarum vīṟṟiruppar.
amerikka aviyiṉ nili ivi yaṉittiṟkum māṟāṉatu. irukkikaḷ etiretirāka vaṉṟi orē vaṭṭamāka irukkum. kaṭcikaḷ aracāṅkak kaṭci etirkkaṭci eṉṟu piriyāmal citaṟiyum,uṟuppiṉar oruvarukkoruvar etirāka irāmal parantum iruppar. poruḷāḷar mēṭi pōṉṟa etuvumilli. amiccarō mutalamiccar niliyil oru nāṭṭut talivarō avi etilum vantamarvatēyilli. amaravum kūṭātu. ēṉeṉil iṅkilāntiliruppatu pōl avarō amiccarō avi uṟuppiṉarallar. iṅkilāntil avi ciṟiya iṭattil amintiruppatāl oruvarōṭoruvar vātiṭavō amaḷi ceyyavō iṭamuṇṭu. eṉavē, atu araciyal avi maṭṭumaṉṟu; vāta avi kūṭa. āṉāl amerikka aviyil oruvar pēcuvati oruvar oli perukkiyiṉṟik kēṭka muṭiyātu. vātam uriyāṭal ākiyavaṟṟukku iṭamilli.
aracāṅkatti naṭattum poṟuppuṭiya talivarō amiccarō amerikkāvil avikku varavum muṭiyātu; caṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭu varavum muṭiyātu. avaṟṟiṉ pakarppi avit talivarukku aṉuppuvatuṭaṉ avarkaḷ vēli muṭintuviṭum. aviyār ēṟkiṉṟaṉarā? eṉṟu pārkkavō, ataṉi ātarittup pēcavō, kaṇṭaṉaṅkaḷukku maṟumoḻi kūṟavō avarkaḷāl muṭiyātu. uṇmiyil maṉṟa avi kūṭa accaṭṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟi mikutiyum vātippatilli. ataṉi uruvākka utavuvatumilli. ataṉi uruvākkap pala kaḻakaṅkaḷum(ūṭiaavivananaḷa) tuṇikkaḻakaṅkaḷum(ḷurab-ūṭiaavivanana) pāṭupaṭum. ivaṟṟil pala taṭavi ārāyappaṭṭapiṉpē maṉṟa avikaḷil avi koṇṭu varappaṭum. maṉṟa avi ēṟkāviṭṭāl atu tirutti amikkappaṭuvatilli. putitāka mīṇṭum koṇṭu varappaṭavēṇṭiyatutāṉ.
piriṭṭaṉil aṉṟāṭa naṭimuṟic ceytikaḷ oru kālattil taṉi taṉi uṟuppiṉarāl maṉṟa aviyil koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. ippōtum avvappōtu avi varuvatuṇṭu. āṉāl, perumpālum avi antanta araciyal tuṟiyiṉ paṇiyāḷar kiyilēyē oppaṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. araciyal naṭimuṟittalivar ākiya mutalamiccar maṉṟiṉ perumpāṉmik kaṭci ātaravu peṟṟavarātalāl avarē avaṟṟiṉ poṟuppi ēṟṟu naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, amerikkāvil ovvoru ciṟunaṭimuṟi oḻuṅkukkum talivar araciyal maṉṟatti etirpārttē yākavēṇṭum.
mottamākap pārttāl piriṭṭaṉ araciyal nāṭṭu vaḷarcciyuṭaṉ vaḷarnta oru nāṭṭup paṇpāy eḻuti variyaṟukkappaṭāta niliyil ciṟupāṉmi caṭṭa varampukaḷukkum perumpāṉmi eḻutāc caṭṭamākiya naṭimuṟi marapukaḷ allatu vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkum kaṭṭuppaṭṭa araciyalākum. itaṉi eḻutappaṭā araciyalamippu eṉpar.amerikkāviṉ araciyalamippu itaṟku māṟāka eḻutappaṭṭa araciyalamippu ākum. ataṉ uyirnilip paṇpukaḷ ellām araciyalamippiṉ aṭippaṭic caṭṭamāṉa viṭutali viḷamparattilēyē aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. atilēṟpaṭum ovvoru māṟṟamum caṭṭamūlamē naṭipeṟum. apputuc caṭṭaṅkaḷum araciyal maṉṟāl mutaṟ caṭṭaṅkaḷukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭē niṟivēṟavēṇṭum. eṉavē, amerikka araciyal maṉṟiṉ urimikaḷ muntiya caṭṭaṅkaḷukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭavi. aṭippaṭic caṭṭaṅkaḷi atu māṟṟa muṭiyātu. araciyalamippil iṉṟiyamiyāta māṟṟam vēṇṭum pōtum uṭampaṭikkikaḷ vēṇṭumpōtum kūṭa mēlaviyiṉ mūṉṟiliraṇṭu paṅku uṟuppiṉariṉ iṟutiyiṇakkam peṟavēṇṭum. naṭimuṟi araciyal, maṉṟa avikaḷukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭataṉṟu. āṉāl, āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyal maṉṟam elliyaṟṟa urimiyuṭiyatu. ataṉik kaṭṭuppaṭuttuvatu ataṉ marapum ataṉ viruppamumē tavira vēṟilli. āṅkila araciyal taṉi araciyal; amerikka araciyal ataṉ nāṭṭup pirivukkum vēṟupāṭukaḷukkum ēṟṟapaṭi kūṭṭu araciyalāy amintuḷḷatu.
āṅkila araciyal kuṭiyāṭci, poṟuppāṭci ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uḷḷārnta taṉmikaḷuṭiyatāyiṉum veḷit tōṟṟattilēṉum muṭiyāṭci, variyaṟippaṭṭa muṭiyāṭci, kuḻuvāṭci vallunarāṭci ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu tikaḻvatu. amerikka araciyal kalappaṟṟa kuṭiyāṭci amerikka makkaḷiṉ viṭutaliyārvarvattil caṭṭeṉa malarnta ōviyamalar atu; āṅkilanāṭṭu maṉṟattippōla atu kālattiṉ pōkkil mella muḷittuk kāḻttu ili taḻittu, arumpi mukiḻttu malarnta vaḷarcciyum uyirppum uṭiya malar aṉṟu. āyiṉum aḵtu oruvakiyil vāṭātu perumpālum orē niliyil irukkum malarē. amerikkar āṅkila araciyali avarkaḷ pārāṭṭiṉum, tam araciyalē muḻu viṭutaliyārvam niṟinta muḻukkuṭiyāṭci eṉak koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
amerikkak kuṭiyaracil āṭciyurimikaḷ perumpālum iru kūṟukaḷāyp pirikkappaṭṭa iraṭṭi āṭci muṟiyē yākum. oru kūṟu vāḻviyal (camūka) paṇpukaḷiṉ mītu āṭci celuttuvatu. itu perumpālum taṉiyaracukaḷiṭamē yuḷḷatu. iṉṉoru kūṟu poruḷiyal vāḻvukku iṉṟiyamiyāta tanti, añcal, pōkkuvaravu, toḻil amippu, veḷināṭṭut toṭarpu, taṉiyaraciyalkaḷ toṭarpu ākiyavi paṟṟiyatu. ikkūṟu peritum kūṭṭuṟavu araciyaliṉ poṟuppiluḷḷatu. taṉi araciyalkaḷ uṇmiyil pēraḷavil taṟcārpu uṭiyavi.  kūṭṭuṟavu araciyal nēraṭiyāka makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkapaṭṭa āṭci, peyarmaṉṟamum kūṭṭuṟavut talivarum uṭiyatāyiṉum annili ōraḷavu mēlaviyiṉ urimikaḷāl kaṭṭuppaṭuttap paṭukiṟatu. immēlavi aṭippaṭic caṭṭaṅkaḷit taṭuttu niṟuttum urimi uṭiyatu.  ellā araciyalkaḷiliruntum carinikar peyarāṇmiyuṭiya itaṉ mūṉṟiliraṇṭu perum pāṉmi uṟuppiṉariṉṟi uṭampaṭikkiyō taliciṟanta caṭṭaṅkaḷō ēṟpaṭamuṭiyātu. eṉavē, āṅkila nāṭṭip pōl mēlavi amerikkāvil araciyalamippiṉ aṇiyāka maṭṭum amiyāmal uṇmiyilēyē talimiyāṉa urimikaḷ uṭiyatāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
āṅkila nāṭṭil aṇmivari iru araciyal kaṭcikaḷē iruntaṉa. taṟkālattil mūṉṟāvatākat toḻiṟkaṭci oṉṟu muṉṉaṇikku vantirukkiṟatu. āyiṉum araciyalamippu vakiyil aracāṅkak kaṭci etirkkaṭci eṉṟa pākupāṭu kuliyavilli. mūṉṟām kaṭci iraṇṭil etaṉuṭaṉāvatu nilavaramākac cērntō avvappōtu nilimāṟiyō araciyal vaṭṭāṭṭattiṟkuc cuvi tarukiṟatu. amerikkāvil toṭakkakattil  uṇmiyil kaṭcikaḷuk kiṭamillāmal palvakik kuḻukkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruntaṉa. āyiṉum nāḷaṭivil kuṭiyāṭcikkaṭci(cunayīraṭeiuyaḷē) potu āṭcikkaṭci(ṉunaaṭiucayavaḷa) eṉṉum irukaṭcikaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. toḻilāḷar kuḻu oru kaṭciyāka muṉṉaṇikku varavilli. kāraṇam amerikkāvil putukkāṭukaḷ niṟintiruppatāl avaṟṟit tiruttip payiriṭum kuṭiyāṉavar peruntokiyiṉar. toḻil mutalāḷikaḷ perutta kiḻakku araciyal pakutikaḷ kuṭiyēṟṟak kaṭciyiyum; paṇṇi mutalāḷikaḷ kuṭiyāṉavar vāṇikak kuḻuviṉar niṟinta teṟku, mēṟku, vaṭamēṟku araciyal pakutikaḷ potu āṭciyiyum ātarikkiṉṟaṉa. ikkaṭcikaḷē taṉittaṉi iyaṅkum araciyalkaḷiyum kūṭṭuṟaviyum orupuṟamum, caṭṭa amippu maṉṟaṅkaḷiyum naṭimuṟi araciyaliyum iṉṉoru puṟamum iṇikka utavukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ muyaṟciyāl mutalil perunilak kiḻavar kiyilirunta amerikka araciyal ippōtu perumpālum ciṟunilakkiḻavar kikku māṟiyuḷḷatu.
7. airōppiya āciya nāṭukaḷiṉ- 
araciyal vaḷarcci
airōppiya nāṭukaḷiṉ araciyal vaḷarcci 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭu iṟuti variyum kiṭṭattaṭṭa nārmaṉ aracar āṭcit toṭakkattil iṅkilāntilirunta nilimiyilēyē iruntatu. mutal eṭvarṭu kālattil iṅkilāntil ēṟpaṭṭatu pōṉṟa mummaṇṭalap pirivu (talimakkaḷ maṇṭalam, perumakkaḷ maṇṭalam, potumakkaḷ maṇṭalam eṉṟa pākupāṭu) atē camayattil pirāṉcilum ēṟpaṭṭiruntatu. āyiṉum āṟṟal mikka aracar āṭcik kālaṅkaḷil atu kūṭṭappaṭāmalō allatu ṭiyūṭar aracar kālattil iruntatu pōl aracar kippāviyākavō iruntatu. eṭṭām heṉriyip pōlavē taṉṉāṇmi mikka aracaṉākiya patiṉāṉkām lūyiyiṉ āṭciyil atu kūṭṭappeṟāmalē pōyiṟṟu. pirañcup puraṭciyiṉ pōtu atu urukkulintu palappala veṟitta māṟutalkaḷaṭintatu.
pirāṉcilumcari, airōppiya nāṭukaḷ piṟavaṟṟilum cari, neppōliyaṉ āṭcikkāla araciyal puyaliṟpaṭṭu ippaḻiya araciyal muṟṟilum cīrkulivuṟṟu aḻintu pōyiṟṟu. airōppāvil ēṟpaṭṭa taṟkāla araciyal muṟikaḷ ellāmē in neppōliyaṉ kālap puraṭcikkup piṟpaṭṭaṉavēyākum. appuraṭciyiṟpaṭṭu aḻiyāmalum veḷippaṭiyākavēṉum ataṉ tākkutalukku uṭpaṭāmalum irunta araciyal, piriṭṭiṣ araciyal oṉṟē. eṉavētāṉ avi yaṉittum veḷiyamip pilēṉum iṅkilāntiṉ araciyalip piṉpaṟṟi yaviyā yuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ kāṇum potuoṟṟumi iru avikaḷ iruppatum, naṭimuṟikaḷ peritum mēlīṭāka ottiruppatumē yākum. āyiṉum avi oṉṟilēṉum mēlavi iṅkilāntip pōla vaḻivaḻip piṟappurimi uṭiyatu aṉṟu. pirāṉcu, pelciyam, hālantu, svīṭaṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷil mēlavi uṟuppiṉar kīḻaviyip pōlavē tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. nārvēyil atu kīḻaviyāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa oru kuḻu āka amintuḷḷatu. ittāliyilō ataṉ uṟuppiṉar maṉṉarāl kīḻavikkup poṟuppuṭiya amiccar aṟivuriyuṭaṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻumikkumākat tērnteṭukkappaṭṭu vantaṉar.
mēlum pirāṉcu, ittāli, pelciyam, hālantu ākiya nāṭukaḷil piriṭṭiṣ araciyaliṉ veḷiyuruvam maṭṭumallāmal ōraḷavu ataṉ taṉmikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittāliyilum pelciyattilum hālantilum iṅkilāntip pōlavē muṭiyāṭci ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mutaliraṇṭum maṉṉarālum iṟutināṭu araciyalum āḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ aracarō araciyō iṅkilāntu maṉṉarip pōlavē kīḻaviyil perumpāṉmik kaṭciyiliruntu terinteṭukkap paṭṭa  poṟuppuṭiya amiccar kuḻu mūlam āḷukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē annāṭukaḷiṉ āṭci poṟuppu vāynta muṭiyāṭci (ūṭiḷēvaviraviṭiyēṭa aṭiyēcuhla) ākum. pirāṉcilum aracaṉukku iṇiyāka iṭampeṟṟuḷḷa talivar itupōlavē poṟupṭiya amiccar kuḻumūlam āṭciceluttukiṉṟār. eṉavē atuvum poṟuppu vāynta kuṭiyāṭciyē yākum.
āṉāl mēṟkūṟiya nāṭukaḷukku māṟāka (nāciyar āṭcivari) cermaṉiyilum āsṭriyā haṅkēriyilum (nāḷatuvari) sviṭcarlāntilum piriṭṭaṉ pōṉṟa poṟuppāṭci illi. atōṭu araciyalamippiṉ mātiriyum iṅkilāntip pōṉṟa taṉiyaraciyal muṟiyā yirāmal amerikkāvip pōṉṟa kūṭṭuṟavu araciyal muṟikaḷā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. kūṭṭuṟavu vakimaṭṭum ovvoṉṟilum ovvoru muṟiyil vēṟupaṭṭu irukkiṉṟatu.
kirīs 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavum taṉippaṭṭa araciyal vāḻvi iḻantu mutal ulakap pōrukkuppiṉ viṭutali peṟṟu āṅkila aracar niliyi otta aracar āṭciyip peṟṟatu.
raṣyā mutal ulakappōrvari ṭiyūṭar āṭci pōṉṟa taṉṉāṇmi araciyaluṭaṉē iruntu tiṭumeṉa raṣyap puraṭci mūlam oru putiya vakik kūṭṭuṟavu araciyal muṟiyit taṉakkeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭatu. piriṭṭaṉ araciyal talimiyi mīṟi ataṉit tāṇṭi araciyalil pututtuṟi vakutta nāṭu iṉṟu ulakil raṣyā oṉṟē. ataṉi iṉṉoru pirivil kavaṉippōm.
āciya nāṭukaḷil pala taṉṉāṇmiyiḻantu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu cila valiyiḻantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. mēli nāṭukaḷukkoppāka vaḷarntuḷḷa cappāṉ uṇmiyil ṭiyūṭar āṭci pōṉṟa taṉṉāṇmimikka muṭiyāṭciyiyē iraṇṭām ulakappōr muṭivuvari koṇṭiruntatu. āyiṉum atu veḷi vaṭivilēṉum piriṭṭaṉip pōṉṟa amiccar kuḻuvum maṉṟa avikaḷum naṭimuṟikaḷum vakuttuḷḷatu. cīṉā pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari paḻiya pēraracar āṭciyiliruntu,piṉ veḷināṭṭār curaṇṭalālum tākkutalālum pēraracar valimikkuṟivālum cīrkeṭṭatu. inniliyil aṟiñar cuṉyatcēṉum avarukkuppiṉa ciyāṅ-ki-ṣēkkum tōṉṟi ataṉi oru kuṭiyaracākac cīramittu vantaṉar. raṣyap potuvuṭimiccārpiṉarkaḷum cīṉāviṉ oru pakutiyil taṉiyāka aracu niṟuviyuḷḷārkaḷ.
pāracīka nāṭu, āpkaṉittāṉam ākiya paḻiya nāṭukaḷ muṭiyaracukaḷ. avi piriṭṭaṉiṉ mātiriyi mēl pōkkāka vēṉum piṉpaṟṟa muyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. turukkip pērara ciṉiṉṟu viṭupaṭṭa īrāk, ciriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ iṉṉum piriṭṭaṉiyō pirāṉciyē  cārntē vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvum atupōlavē turukkiyō kamāl pāṣāviṉ oppaṟṟa talimiyiṉ kīḻ piriṭṭaṉ pōṉṟa niliyil kuṭiyaracāy viṭṭatu.
iṉip piriṭṭaṉi ōraḷavu muṉ mātiriyākak koṇṭa airōppiya nāṭukaḷiṉ araciyalamippukaḷik kavaṉippōm.
pirañcup puraṭcikkup piṉ ēṟpaṭṭa pirañcuk kuṭiyāṭciyiṉ puṟavaṭivam uṇmiyil piriṭṭaṉiṉ mātiriyip piṉpaṟṟi eḻuntatē. āṅkila araciyal maṉṟattiṉ oḻuṅku muṟikaḷ rāmilli(cuṭiaṭiṭala) eṉpavar moḻipeyarttiruntār. immoḻi peyarppip pentamiṉ tōḻarāyirunta ṭumāṇṭ mūlam peṟṟu mirāpō eṉṟa araciyal talivar ataṉip puraṭcikkālap pēravi muṉ vittār. avi āṅkila oḻuṅkumuṟi namakkēṉ eṉṟu ataṉip puṟakkaṇittuviṭṭatu. āyiṉum patiṉeṭṭām lūyiyiṉ kālattil mīṭṭum muṭiyāṭci ēṟpaṭṭa pōtu immoḻipeyarppu upayōkap paṭuttik koḷḷappaṭṭatām.
pirañcup puraṭcik kālattil aṟiñar kaṟpaṉi ulakukaḷiṭiyē karuppeṟṟu makkaḷ uḷḷattil urampeṟṟu eḻunta mūṉṟu aṭippaṭikkōṭpāṭukaḷ cari oppunili, uṭaṉpiṟantār nili, taṟcārpu nili (kucayavanacanvila, ṭuinecavala, nuḷūrayaṭavila) ākiya mūṉṟumē uṇmiyil (jār aracar kāla raṣyā nīṅkalāka) vēṟenta nāṭṭiyum viṭa ip puraṭcik kālattukku muntiya pirāṉciltāṉ immūṉṟum maruntukkum illāta nilimiyiliruntatu. itaṉi pirāṉciṉ araciyal iruṭkālam allatu vallāṭcik kālam eṉṟu kūṟalām. iraṇṭām pakuti puraṭcit toṭakkamākiya 1785 mutal 1875 variyuḷḷa ārāyvu nilik kālamākum. ikkālattil pala araciyalamippukaḷ oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāka vantu kiyāḷap paṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟi amittavar āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyaliliruntu tām puṉintu perukkiya kaṉāvulakak karuttukkaḷi vallāṭcikkeṉa amikkappaṭṭa araciyal caṭṭattiṉuḷ tiṇikka muyaṉṟu avarkaḷ perumpālum tōlviyē yaṭintaṉar. iṟutiyil kaṉavukaḷi yellām kiviṭṭut taṟkālikamāka naṭimuṟit tiṭṭamāka ellāk kaṭcikkum caṟṟu viṭṭuk koṭukkum ‘aviyal’ tiṭṭamoṉṟu koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. itaṉi amittavarkaḷ itaṉit taṟkālika muṟiyākak koṇṭu variṉum itu kiṭṭattaṭṭa nilavaramāṉa araciyalāy amintatu. ēṉeṉil itu camayattukkēṟṟapaṭi vaḷarcciyum māṟupāṭum ēṟpaṭuvataṟku iṭantantu toyvuṭiyatāyiruntatu. 
mūṉṟāvatu kālam 1875 mutal iraṇṭām ulakap pōr muṭivuvari itaṉi vaḷarccik kālam eṉṉalām.
puraṭcik kālattil mūṉṟu amippukkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa mutalāvatu 1789il maṉita urimikaḷi vakuttatu. 1791l poṟuppuṭiya amiccar kuḻuvum ōraviyuṭiya maṉṟum amittatu. 1795l uṭimivariyaṟiyuṭaṉ moḻittērvu naṭāttit kūṭṭap peṟṟa īravi maṉṟamum ataṉāl terinteṭukkappaṭṭa naṭimuṟik kuḻuviṉar aivarum aminta mūṉṟām araciyalamippu ēṟpaṭṭatu. 4 āṇṭukaḷ kaḻiyumuṉ 1799-il neppōliyaṉi mutalvaṉākak koṇṭa kuṭiyaracu niṟuvappaṭṭatu. 1800-il araciyal maṉṟiṉ urimikaḷik kuṟittu naṭimuṟiyāḷar valimiyi uyartti neppōliyaṉ taṉ talimiyil āṭci muṟi amittu muṭiyēṟṟup pēraraciyum niṟuviṉāṉ.
puraṭcikkārar viṭutalik kaṉavu pirañcu makkaḷ uḷḷattilum avarkaḷ nāṭṭu vāḻvilum varalāṟṟilum evvaḷavu patintaṉavō atiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku mika āḻamāy neppōliyaṉ pēraracuk kaṉavukaḷum avaṉ āṭci muṟikaḷum irāṇuva muṟikaḷum patintu vērūṉṟiṉa. avaṉ amipput tiṟattiṉ payaṉākap puraṭcik kuḻappattāl valiyaṟṟirunta taṉṉāṭci niliyaṅkaḷ mīṭṭum uyirppikkap peṟṟu uyar araciyaluṭaṉ naṉku piṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. puraṭcik kālattil akaṟṟappaṭṭa perumakkaḷ mīṭṭum niliniṟuvap paṭṭaṉar. nāṭṭu urimip paṭi eṉṟa matippuk kuḻuvum(ṭunapiṭin ṭika ḻaṭinṭiraca) amikkappaṭṭatu. ulakil neppōliyaṉ oppaṟṟa vāḷ vīraṉākavē karutappaṭiṉum uṇmiyil avaṉ oppaṟṟa āṭci vīraṉum āvaṉ. avaṉ vāḷ valimiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa aracu pōṉa piṉṉum avaṉ eḻutukōl vaṉmiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa nallāṭci muṟikaḷ iṉṟaḷavum nilittuḷḷaṉa.
tōlviyaṟiyā neppōliyaṉ iṟutip pōril paṭuvīḻcciyaṭintapiṉ 1815-il mīṭṭum paḻiyapūrpaṉ aracar muṭiyēṟṟaṉar. ivar āṅkila nāṭṭu muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟa vīṇil muyaṉṟaṉar. ittōlvi maṉṉar tiṟamiṉmiyiṉ payaṉē eṉṟu eṇṇi 1830-il ārliyaṉs marapil vanta iṉṉoru maṉṉarik koṇṭa immuṟiyi naṭattip pārttaṉar. ituvum payaṉaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. 1848-il naṭipeṟṟa iraṇṭāvatu puraṭciyiṉ pōtu amerikkāvip pōl pala pakutikaḷiṉ āṭpeyarkaḷum vantu kūṭittērnteṭukkappaṭṭa orē aviyum 4 āṇṭukku oru muṟi vayatu vanta ellā makkaḷālum tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa talivarum uṭiya kuṭiyaracu niṟuvap peṟṟatu. āṉāl, itaṉi naṭattat takka āṟṟal vāynta talivaṉillā niliyil neppōliyaṉ kuṭiyil vanta lūyi neppōliyaṉ ki āṭciyik koṇṭu iraṇṭām pēraraci niṟuviṉāṉ itu 1852mutal 1870 vari nīṭittatu. ik kālattil araciyal vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭāta pōtilum amiti nilaviṟṟu. nāṭṭiṉ celvamum celvākkum vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa. itaṉ piṟpakutiyil pala pōrkaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟāl āṭci ciṟitu nalivuṟṟatu. iṟutiyil piraṣyāvuṭaṉ (atāvatu cermaṉiyuṭaṉ) naṭipeṟṟa pōril pirāṉcu tōṟṟu alsās, loreyiṉ mutaliya kittoḻil mākāṇaṅkaḷi iḻakka nērntatu. itaṉuṭaṉ iraṇṭām pēraracu vīḻcci yaṭintatu.
itaṉpiṉ mūṉṟāvatu taṭaviyum oru kuṭiyaracu ēṟpaṭṭatu. muṭiyāṭcik kaṭciyiṉarukkum veṟitta kuṭiyaracuk kaṭciyiṉarāṉa potu uṭimik kaṭciyiṉarukkum uḷnāṭṭup pōr ēṟpaṭṭatu. ik kuḻappa niliyil kaṭcikaḷip pakikkātu iṇittuk kuṭiyāṭcik kaṭciyiṉarāṉa ṭiyarsum makmōkaṉum 1875-i l taṟkālimāka ōr araciyali niṟuviṉar. itu uṇmiyil mūṉṟu vēṟu vēṟu maṉitar ēṟpaṭuttiya araciyal caṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ kūṭṭaviyalē yākum. āyiṉum mēṟkuṟippiṭṭapaṭi enta uyar araciyalum nilipeṟāta aḷavu atu ataṉ nekiḻcciyiṉ payaṉāka nilipeṟṟatu.
toṇṇūṟāṇṭukaḷākap pala araciyal amippu kaḷiyum kiyāṇṭu kiyāṇṭu pārtta pirañcu makkaḷ iṟutiyil,veṟṟi araciyal amippiṉ niṟiviṉālaṉṟu, atu vaḷarccikku iṭam tarum muṟiyil amivataṉālēyē eṉṟu kaṇṭu koṇṭaṉar.
pirañcu araciyalamippu oru vakiyil piriṭṭiṣ muṟikku māṟāy amerikka muṟiyi ottatu. atu nāḷaṭivil vaḷarcciyaṭinta uruvāyirāmal orē āṇṭil orē maṉitaṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa aṭippaṭi mītu eḻuntatu ākum. āyiṉum atu amerikkāvip pōlavum muṟṟilum illi. amerikkāvil araciyalamippu oru caṭṭatti aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭatu. pirāṉcil atu 3 caṭṭaṅkaḷi aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭatu. ataṉōṭu atu amerikka araciyali viṭa mikavum nekiḻcciyu muṭiyatu. araciyalamippil māṟṟaṅ koṇṭu varutal iṅkē mikavum eḷitu. iraṇṭu avikaḷum iṇinta maṉṟak kūṭṭattil niṟiṟēṟṟappaṭṭāl araciyal amippil ettakiya cīrtiruttamum ceyyalākum. āyiṉum uṇmiyil 1875kkup piṉ 3 taṭaviyē ic cīrtiruttam ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu iv araciyalamippiṉ uṟutikku oru cāṉṟu ākum. 
pirāṉcil kuṭiyaracut talivar iṭam āṅkila maṉṉar iṭattippōṉṟē periya matippukkuriyatu. āṉāl atip pōlavē naṭimuṟiyil taṉippaṭṭa urimikaḷ aṟṟatu. iraṇṭu avikaḷum kūṭiya maṉṟak kūṭṭattil taṉippaṭṭa perumpāṉmi moḻikaḷ mūlam avar tērnteṭukkap paṭukiṟār. pirāṉcil iraṇṭu avikaḷum perumpālum vēṟuvēṟu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilēyē kūṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlavi(ḷunayēvana) laksampark araṇmaṉiyilum(ñayaṭayaḷi ṭuronaarecapa) āṭpeyaravi(ūhyaaneca ṭika ṉunayīravananaḷa) pūrpaṉ araṇmaṉiyilum (ñayaṭayaḷi kṣaṭiracabṭin) kūṭum. tērnteṭukkappaṭṭapiṉ talivar āṭci ēḻu āṇṭukaḷ vari cellupaṭiyākum. ataṉ muṭivukku oru tiṅkaḷiṉ muṉ talivar iraṇṭavikaḷiyum kūṭṭa vēṇṭum. talivar iṭam tiṭumeṉa nirappa vēṇṭiya viṭattum atu kūṭum.
tērtalkaḷil niṟkum kaṭcikaḷ palavātalāl avi oṉṟiraṇṭu kūṭṭuk kaṭcikaḷākac cērntu potuc cārpāka āṭkaḷi niṟuttum. ovvōr uṟuppiṉarum pala kaṭcikaḷukku ukantavarāyirukka vēṇṭiya varātalāl kūṭiya maṭṭum ellārukkum potuvāṉa koḷkikaḷiyē ceyalāṟṟuvar. eṉavē araciyaliṉ pōkku veṟitta muṟpōkkuṭiyatā yirukkavō allatu ceyalārvamikkatā yirukkavō iṭamilli.
iṅkilāntiviṭa amerikkāvilum pirāṉcilum mēlavikku āṟṟal mikuti. pirañcu mēlaviyil 300 uṟuppiṉar uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ 9 āṇṭuk kālattiṟkut tērnteṭukkappaṭuvar. tērtalum āṭ peyaravic cārpil maṟimukamākavē naṭipeṟum. ovvōrāṇṭum mūṉṟiloru paṅku uṟuppiṉar nīkkip putuppikkappeṟuvar. ivvavi poruḷiyal cārpāṉa naṭimuṟikaḷit toṭaṅki vikka muṭiyāviṭiṉum avaṟṟit tiruttavō avaṟṟiṉ mītu vātāṭavō kūṭum.
āṭpeyaraviyil uṟuppiṉar toki 584. ivarkaḷ kuṭimakkaḷiṭiya nēraṭiyāka 4 āṇṭuk kālattukkut tērnteṭukkappaṭuvar. avikkaṭṭiṭam piriṭṭaṉiluḷḷati viṭap peritākavum nāṭakak koṭṭakipōl amiti vaṭiviṉatākavum irukkiṟatu. piṟi vaḷiviṉ naṭuvil amiccarkaḷum irupuṟattuḷ valatupuṟattil ātarikkum kaṭcikaḷum iṭatu puṟattil ātarikkāta kaṭcikaḷum irukkum. vaḷivetiril mēṭimītu kuṭiyaracut talivar iṭamum ataṉ piṉ eḻuttāḷar irukkikaḷum mēṭiyiṉ kīḻ uṟuppiṉar vantu pēcum ciṟu cāymēṭiyum irukkum.
araciyal naṭimuṟiyil ivvamippu iru kaṭci araciyali viṭak kaṭci iṇippu araciyalukkē peritum ukantatu. eṉavē araciyal māṟṟam ēṟpaṭumpōtu perumpālum enta amiccar kuḻuvum muṟṟilum māṟuvatilli. ātaravu kiviṭṭa kaṭciyiṉiṭamāka vēṟukaṭci āṭkaḷ cēra atu mīṭṭum tiruttiyamikkappaṭuvatē perumpāṉmi nikaḻcci yākum. maṉṟu naṭimuṟiyil piriṭṭaṉip pōla vātaṅkaḷ irā. āṉāl uṟuppiṉarkaḷ naṭuvil vantu vācippataṉāl tiruttamāka eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiya niṟivuṭiya pēruri kaḷukku iṭamuṇṭu.
pirāṉcil araciyal talivar eḷitil nīkkappaṭa muṭiyātu. avarnili piriṭṭiṣ aracar nilipōṉṟatu. āṉāl amiccarkaḷi nampikkiyillāt tīrmāṉa mūlam akaṟṟalām. iv vamiccar poṟuppup piriṭṭaṉip piṉpaṟṟi amintatē yākum.
